  ### Connection 改动清单

  4. **C-S1**: 删除 core/clone.ts
     - ⚠️ 此文件有 16 处 conditional spread（省略 undefined key）
     - structuredClone 会保留 undefined key，语义不同
     - **策略**: 不删文件，改为内部统一用 `{ ...obj }` 浅拷贝 + 对嵌套对象逐层 spread
     - 或者保留文件只删除冗余的 conditional spread（改为简单 spread，因为 TypeScript 类型已保证字段存在性）
     - 逐函数确认：哪些 conditional spread 保护的是真正可能 undefined 的字段 vs 类型已保证必填的字段

  5. **C-S2**: 删除 selectors/ 目录

     - 更新 connection/index.ts 不再 export selector 函数，只 export 类型

     - connect(config: unknown) → connect(config: TransportConfig)
     - 内部 normalizeTransportConfig 不变

  7. **C-R2**: 原子化 connect
     - 当前流程：先 upsertConfig → 再 adapter.connect → 失败后幽灵 config 留在 state
     - 改为：先 adapter.connect → 成功后 upsertConfig + applyEvent
     - 失败时不写任何 state，直接返回失败结果

     - ConnectionTransportAdapter 接口新增可选 discoverResources?() 方法
     - ConnectionService 新增 discoverResources() 方法，委托给 adapter
     - 无 adapter 支持时返回空数组

  9. **C-R4**: BaseTransportConfig 新增 autoConnect?: boolean

  **Connection 完成后**: `pnpm -C rewrite vitest run` 确认全绿

## Frame Feature Files Report

I've successfully read all 8 files from the Frame feature's validation, legacy, and service layers. Here's a comprehensive analysis:

---

### 1. **validation-expression.ts** (191 lines)

**Purpose**: Validates expression definitions used in frame fields.

**Key Functions**:
- `validateExpressionDefinition()` - Main validation function for expression configurations
- `validateExpressionSyntax()` - Checks expression syntax and security
- `validateVariable()` - Validates expression variable definitions
- `validateConditionalExpression()` - Validates conditional expressions
- `extractExpressionIdentifiers()` - Extracts variable identifiers from expressions

**Key Features**:
- Security checks against dangerous patterns (eval, Function, etc.)
- Expression compilation validation
- Variable identifier validation and duplicate detection
- Support for frame field and external variable sources

---

### 2. **validation-field.ts** (106 lines)

**Purpose**: Validates individual frame field definitions.

**Key Functions**:
- `validateFrameField()` - Main field validation function

**Key Features**:
- Validates field ID, name, data type, and length
- Fixed data type length validation (uint8=1, uint16=2, etc.)
- Input type validation (text, select, radio, expression)
- Data participation type validation
- Options validation for select/radio fields
- Integration with expression validation for expression input types

---

### 3. **validation-frame.ts** (118 lines)

**Purpose**: Validates complete frame assets and collections.

**Key Functions**:

- Collection-level duplicate ID detection
- Integration with field validation

**Key Features**:

- Enum validation for all typed fields

- `createFrameAssetService()` - Creates full frame service

## Comprehensive Analysis of Connection Feature Files

I've read all 9 requested files. Here's the complete analysis:

---

### 1. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/core/clone.ts`
**Lines:** 127  
**Purpose:** Deep cloning utilities for connection state immutability

**Key Exports:**
- `cloneTransportConfig()` - Clones transport configuration objects
- `cloneTransportConfigs()` - Clones arrays of transport configs
- `cloneConnectionCounters()` - Clones counter snapshots
- `cloneTransportTarget()` - Clones transport target snapshots
- `cloneTransportError()` - Clones error snapshots
- `cloneTransportEvent()` - Clones event snapshots
- `cloneConnectionRuntimeFact()` - Clones runtime fact objects
- `cloneConnectionStateSnapshot()` - Clones entire connection state

**Notable:** Handles all 4 transport types (serial, tcp-client, tcp-server, udp) with specific field copying for each.

---

### 2. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/core/types.ts`
**Lines:** 189  
**Purpose:** Core type definitions and constants

**Key Constants:**
- `CONNECTION_SCHEMA_VERSION = 2`
- `TRANSPORT_KINDS = ['serial', 'tcp-client', 'tcp-server', 'udp']`
- `CONNECTION_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES = ['idle', 'connecting', 'connected', 'disconnecting', 'disconnected', 'error']`
- `TRANSPORT_EVENT_KINDS = ['connect-requested', 'connected', 'disconnect-requested', 'disconnected', 'cleanup', 'data', 'write-requested', 'write-accepted', 'write-failed', 'error', 'stale-event', 'reconnect-scheduled', 'reconnect-exhausted']`
- `TRANSPORT_ERROR_KINDS = ['resource-unavailable', 'open-failed', 'connect-failed', 'listen-failed', 'bind-failed', 'write-failed', 'timeout', 'close-failed', 'stale-event', 'invalid-config']`
- `TRANSPORT_TARGET_ROLES = ['serial-port', 'tcp-client-peer', 'tcp-server-listener', 'tcp-server-client', 'udp-remote']`

**Key Interfaces:**
- `SerialTransportConfig`, `TcpClientTransportConfig`, `TcpServerTransportConfig`, `UdpTransportConfig`
- `TransportTargetSnapshot` - Target representation with availability
- `TransportErrorSnapshot` - Error with recoverability flag
- `ConnectionCounterSnapshot` - Statistics (connectAttempts, rxBytes, txBytes, etc.)
- `TransportEventSnapshot` - Event with optional payload/error/reconnect info
- `ConnectionRuntimeFact` - Runtime state per connection
- `ConnectionStateSnapshot` - Global state with configs, facts, events
- `NormalizedTransportEventInput` - Internal event format

---

### 3. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/core/index.ts`
**Lines:** 68  
**Purpose:** Public API barrel file for core module

**Key Exports:**
- Constants: `CONNECTION_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES`, `CONNECTION_SCHEMA_VERSION`, `TRANSPORT_ERROR_KINDS`, `TRANSPORT_EVENT_KINDS`, `TRANSPORT_KINDS`, `TRANSPORT_TARGET_ROLES`
- All types from `./types`
- Clone functions from `./clone`
- Lifecycle functions: `createDisconnectedRuntimeFact()`, `createEmptyConnectionCounters()`, `createEmptyConnectionSnapshot()`, `createTransportEventSnapshot()`, `deriveTransportTarget()`, `reduceTransportEvent()`, `upsertConnectionConfig()`
- Validation: `createConnectionIssue()`, `createConnectionValidationOutcome()`, `normalizeTransportConfig()`, `validateTransportConfig()`
- Reconnect: `getReconnectPolicy()`, `nextReconnectDelay()`, `shouldReconnect()`

---

### 4. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/core/lifecycle.ts`
**Lines:** 312  
**Purpose:** State machine logic for connection lifecycle

**Key Functions:**
- `createEmptyConnectionCounters()` - Initializes zeroed counters
- `createEmptyConnectionSnapshot()` - Creates empty state
- `deriveTransportTarget()` - Derives target from config
- `createDisconnectedRuntimeFact()` - Creates initial runtime fact
- `createTransportEventSnapshot()` - Normalizes events
- `upsertConnectionConfig()` - Adds/updates config, updates/creates runtime fact
- `reduceTransportEvent()` - Core reducer applying events to state

**Event Limit:** 50 events max in snapshot

**Lifecycle Logic:**
- Events drive lifecycle transitions: connect-requested→connecting→connected, disconnect-requested→disconnecting→disconnected, etc.
- Availability set true only on 'connected' events
- Counters updated per event type
- Reconnect state (attempt/nextAt) reset on 'connected', set on 'reconnect-scheduled'

---

### 5. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/selectors/connection-selectors.ts`
**Lines:** 143  
**Purpose:** Query functions for connection state

**Key Exports:**
- `selectConnectionSnapshot()` - Full state clone
- `selectTransportConfigs()` - All configs
- `selectConnectionFacts()` - All runtime facts
- `selectConnectionFact(id)` - Single fact by ID
- `selectConnectionSummaries()` - Derived ConnectionSummary[]
- `selectTransportTargets(query?)` - Filtered targets by kind/availability
- `selectLastTransportError()` - Global last error
- `selectTransportEvents()` - All events

**Types:**
- `ConnectionTargetQuery` - Filter by kind/availableOnly
- `ConnectionSummary` - High-level connection view (id, kind, lifecycle, label, routeLabel, available, rxBytes, txBytes, errorCount, lastActivityAt, lastError)
- `ReconnectStatus` - Reconnect state (isActive, attempt, nextAt, policy)

---

### 6. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/selectors/index.ts`

---

### 7. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/services/connection-service.ts`
**Lines:** 486  
**Purpose:** Main service orchestrating connections, adapters, and state

**Key Interfaces:**
- `ConnectionReader` - Read-only access (10 query methods)
- `ConnectionService` - Extends reader with mutations (connect, disconnect, write, drainAdapterEvents, cleanup)
- `ConnectionOperationOutcome` - Result with ok, validation, snapshot, events, error?
- `CreateConnectionServiceOptions` - Adapter, optional state container, optional now()

**Key Functions:**
- `createConnectionReader()` - Creates read-only facade
- `createConnectionService()` - Creates full service with reconnect logic

**Reconnect Implementation:**
- Timer-based reconnection with exponential backoff
- `scheduleReconnect()` - Sets timeout with policy-based delay
- `handleReconnectTimerFired()` - Attempts reconnect, reschedules on failure
- `clearReconnectTimer()` / `clearAllReconnectTimers()` - Cleanup
- Reconnect triggered on 'disconnected' events if policy enabled and attempts not exhausted

**Event Flow:**
1. Adapter events normalized via `adapterEventToNormalized()`
2. Applied to state via `state.applyEvent()`
3. Reconnect logic checks for disconnects and schedules retries
4. Outcomes include validation, snapshot, events, errors

---

### 8. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/index.ts`
**Lines:** 52  
**Purpose:** Public API barrel for entire connection feature

**Key Exports:**
- **Types:** All transport configs, snapshots, lifecycle, validation from `./core`
- **Selectors:** All 9 selector functions + `ConnectionSummary`, `ConnectionTargetQuery`, `ReconnectStatus`
- **Services:** `createConnectionReader()`, `createConnectionService()` + associated types
- **Adapters:** `createRealSerialAdapter()`, `createRealNetworkAdapter()` + types
- **Reconnect:** `getReconnectPolicy()`, `nextReconnectDelay()`, `shouldReconnect()`, `ReconnectPolicy`

---

### 9. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/adapters/index.ts`
**Lines:** 18  
**Purpose:** Adapter port exports

**Key Exports:**
- **Types:** Connection adapter interfaces (11 types including `ConnectionTransportAdapter`, event/command types, error types)
- **Factories:** `createRealSerialAdapter()`, `createRealNetworkAdapter()`
- **Factory Options:** `CreateRealSerialAdapterOptions`, `CreateRealNetworkAdapterOptions`

---

## Summary Statistics

| File | Lines | Purpose |
|------|-------|---------|
| clone.ts | 127 | Immutability helpers |
| types.ts | 189 | Type definitions |
| index.ts (core) | 68 | Core barrel |
| lifecycle.ts | 312 | State machine logic |
| connection-selectors.ts | 143 | State queries |
| index.ts (selectors) | 13 | Selector barrel |
| connection-service.ts | 486 | Service orchestration |
| index.ts (root) | 52 | Feature barrel |
| adapters/index.ts | 18 | Adapter barrel |
| **Total** | **1,408** | **Full feature** |

## Architecture Observations

1. **Immutable State Pattern:** All mutations return new snapshots via clone functions
2. **Event-Driven:** State updated exclusively via events with normalized format
3. **Transport Abstraction:** 4 transport types (serial, tcp-client, tcp-server, udp)
4. **Lifecycle States:** 6 states (idle, connecting, connected, disconnecting, disconnected, error)
5. **Reconnect Built-In:** Exponential backoff with policy per transport kind
6. **Adapter Pattern:** Pluggable adapters (serial/network) implementing `ConnectionTransportAdapter`
7. **Read-Write Separation:** `ConnectionReader` for queries, `ConnectionService` for mutations
8. **Event Limiting:** Last 50 events retained to prevent unbounded growth

1	import { compileExpression } from '@/shared/expression';
2	import {
3	  EXPRESSION_SOURCE_TYPES,
4	  type ConditionalExpressionDefinition,
5	  type ExpressionDefinition,
6	  type ExpressionVariableDefinition,
7	  type ValidationIssue,
8	  type ValidationResult,
9	} from './types';
10	
11	const EXPRESSION_KEYWORDS = new Set([
12	  'true',
13	  'false',
14	  'null',
15	  'undefined',
16	  'Math',
17	  'sin',
18	  'cos',
19	  'tan',
20	  'log',
21	  'exp',
22	  'sqrt',
23	  'abs',
24	  'floor',
25	  'ceil',
26	  'round',
27	  'max',
28	  'min',
29	  'pow',
30	]);
31	
32	const DANGEROUS_EXPRESSION_PATTERNS: readonly RegExp[] = [
33	  /\beval\s*\(/,
34	  /\bFunction\s*\(/,
35	  /\bsetTimeout\s*\(/,
36	  /\bsetInterval\s*\(/,
37	  /\bwindow\./,
38	  /\bdocument\./,
39	  /\bprocess\./,
40	  /\brequire\s*\(/,
41	  /\bimport\s+/,
42	  /\bexport\s+/,
43	];
44	
45	function createIssue(
46	  code: string,
47	  path: string,
48	  message: string,
49	  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',
50	): ValidationIssue {
51	  return { severity, code, path, message };
52	}
53	
54	function toResult(issues: ValidationIssue[]): ValidationResult {
55	  return {
56	    valid: issues.every((issue) => issue.severity !== 'error'),
57	    issues,
58	  };
59	}
60	
61	function isOneOf<T extends readonly string[]>(value: string, options: T): value is T[number] {
62	  return (options as readonly string[]).includes(value);
63	}
64	
65	function hasText(value: string | undefined): boolean {
66	  return typeof value === 'string' && value.trim().length > 0;
67	}
68	
69	function validateExpressionSyntax(value: string, path: string): ValidationIssue[] {
70	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
71	
72	  if (!hasText(value)) {
73	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.empty', path, '表达式不能为空'));
74	    return issues;
75	  }
76	
77	  const compileResult = compileExpression(value);
78	  if (!compileResult.success) {
79	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.compile', path, `表达式语法错误: ${compileResult.error}`));
80	  }
81	
82	  if (DANGEROUS_EXPRESSION_PATTERNS.some((pattern) => pattern.test(value))) {
83	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.unsafe', path, '表达式包含不允许的运行时代码'));
84	  }
85	
86	  return issues;
87	}
88	
89	function extractExpressionIdentifiers(value: string): string[] {
90	  const matches = value.match(/\b[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*\b/g) ?? [];
91	  return Array.from(new Set(matches.filter((match) => !EXPRESSION_KEYWORDS.has(match))));
92	}
93	
94	function validateVariable(
95	  variable: ExpressionVariableDefinition,
96	  path: string,
97	  seenIdentifiers: Set<string>,
98	): ValidationIssue[] {
99	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
100	
101	  if (!/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$/.test(variable.identifier)) {
102	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.variableIdentifier', path, '变量标识符必须是合法标识符'));
103	  }
104	
105	  if (seenIdentifiers.has(variable.identifier)) {
106	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.variableDuplicate', path, '变量标识符不能重复'));
107	  }
108	  seenIdentifiers.add(variable.identifier);
109	
110	  if (!isOneOf(variable.sourceType, EXPRESSION_SOURCE_TYPES)) {
111	    issues.push(
112	      createIssue('expression.variableSourceUnsupported', path, '变量来源类型不在本轮 pilot 范围内'),
113	    );
114	    return issues;
115	  }
116	
117	  if (variable.sourceType === 'frame_field') {
118	    if (!hasText(variable.frameId)) {
119	      issues.push(createIssue('expression.variableFrameMissing', `${path}.frameId`, '帧字段来源必须指定帧 ID'));
120	    }
121	    if (!hasText(variable.fieldId)) {
122	      issues.push(createIssue('expression.variableFieldMissing', `${path}.fieldId`, '帧字段来源必须指定字段 ID'));
123	    }
124	    return issues;
125	  }
126	
127	  if (!hasText(variable.sourceId) && !hasText(variable.fieldId)) {
128	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.variableSourceMissing', path, '变量来源必须指定 sourceId 或 fieldId'));
129	  }
130	
131	  return issues;
132	}
133	
134	function validateConditionalExpression(
135	  expression: ConditionalExpressionDefinition,
136	  path: string,
137	  variableIdentifiers: Set<string>,
138	): ValidationIssue[] {
139	  const issues = [
140	    ...validateExpressionSyntax(expression.condition, `${path}.condition`),
141	    ...validateExpressionSyntax(expression.expression, `${path}.expression`),
142	  ];
143	
144	  for (const expressionKey of ['condition', 'expression'] as const) {
145	    for (const usedIdentifier of extractExpressionIdentifiers(expression[expressionKey])) {
146	      if (!variableIdentifiers.has(usedIdentifier)) {
147	        issues.push(
148	          createIssue(
149	            'expression.variableUndefined',
150	            `${path}.${expressionKey}`,
151	            `表达式引用了未定义变量 ${usedIdentifier}`,
152	          ),
153	        );
154	      }
155	    }
156	  }
157	
158	  return issues;
159	}
160	
161	export function validateExpressionDefinition(
162	  expression: ExpressionDefinition | undefined,
163	  path = 'expressionConfig',
164	): ValidationResult {
165	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
166	
167	  if (!expression) {
168	    return toResult([createIssue('expression.missing', path, '表达式字段必须配置表达式定义')]);
169	  }
170	
171	  if (!Array.isArray(expression.expressions) || expression.expressions.length === 0) {
172	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.expressionsEmpty', `${path}.expressions`, '至少需要一个表达式'));
173	  }
174	
175	  if (!Array.isArray(expression.variables)) {
176	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.variablesInvalid', `${path}.variables`, '变量映射必须是数组'));
177	    return toResult(issues);
178	  }
179	
180	  const seenIdentifiers = new Set<string>();
181	  for (const [index, variable] of expression.variables.entries()) {
182	    issues.push(...validateVariable(variable, `${path}.variables.${index}`, seenIdentifiers));
183	  }
184	
185	  for (const [index, item] of expression.expressions.entries()) {
186	    issues.push(...validateConditionalExpression(item, `${path}.expressions.${index}`, seenIdentifiers));
187	  }
188	
189	  return toResult(issues);
190	}
191

8	  type ValidationResult,

11	
12	const FIXED_DATA_TYPE_LENGTHS: Partial<Record<FrameDataType, number>> = {
13	  uint8: 1,
14	  int8: 1,
15	  uint16: 2,
16	  int16: 2,
17	  uint32: 4,
18	  int32: 4,
19	  uint64: 8,
20	  int64: 8,
21	  float: 4,
22	  double: 8,
23	};
24	
25	function createIssue(
26	  code: string,
27	  path: string,
28	  message: string,
29	  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',
30	): ValidationIssue {
31	  return { severity, code, path, message };
32	}
33	
34	function toResult(issues: ValidationIssue[]): ValidationResult {
35	  return {
36	    valid: issues.every((issue) => issue.severity !== 'error'),
37	    issues,
38	  };
39	}
40	
41	function isOneOf<T extends readonly string[]>(value: string, options: T): value is T[number] {
42	  return (options as readonly string[]).includes(value);
43	}
44	
45	function hasText(value: string | undefined): boolean {
46	  return typeof value === 'string' && value.trim().length > 0;
47	}
48	
49	export function validateFrameField(
50	  field: FrameFieldDefinition,
51	  path = 'field',
52	): ValidationResult {
53	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
54	
55	  if (!hasText(field.id)) {
56	    issues.push(createIssue('field.idMissing', `${path}.id`, '字段 ID 不能为空'));
57	  }
58	
59	  if (!hasText(field.name)) {
60	    issues.push(createIssue('field.nameMissing', `${path}.name`, '字段名称不能为空'));
61	  }
62	
63	  if (!isOneOf(field.dataType, FRAME_DATA_TYPES)) {
64	    issues.push(createIssue('field.dataTypeUnsupported', `${path}.dataType`, '字段类型不在本轮 pilot 范围内'));
65	  }
66	
67	  if (!Number.isInteger(field.length) || field.length <= 0) {
68	    issues.push(createIssue('field.lengthInvalid', `${path}.length`, '字段长度必须是正整数'));
69	  }
70	
71	  const fixedLength = FIXED_DATA_TYPE_LENGTHS[field.dataType];
72	  if (fixedLength !== undefined && field.length !== fixedLength) {
73	    issues.push(
74	      createIssue(
75	        'field.lengthMismatch',
76	        `${path}.length`,
77	        `字段类型 ${field.dataType} 的长度应为 ${fixedLength}`,
78	      ),
79	    );
80	  }
81	
82	  if (!isOneOf(field.inputType, FRAME_INPUT_TYPES)) {
83	    issues.push(createIssue('field.inputTypeUnsupported', `${path}.inputType`, '字段输入类型不受支持'));
84	  }
85	
86	  if (!isOneOf(field.dataParticipationType, DATA_PARTICIPATION_TYPES)) {
87	    issues.push(
88	      createIssue(
89	        'field.dataParticipationUnsupported',
90	        `${path}.dataParticipationType`,
91	        '数据参与类型不受支持',
92	      ),
93	    );
94	  }
95	
96	  if ((field.inputType === 'select' || field.inputType === 'radio') && field.options.length === 0) {
97	    issues.push(createIssue('field.optionsMissing', `${path}.options`, '选择类字段必须提供选项'));
98	  }
99	
100	  if (field.inputType === 'expression') {
101	    issues.push(...validateExpressionDefinition(field.expressionConfig, `${path}.expressionConfig`).issues);
102	  }
103	
104	  return toResult(issues);
105	}

2	  FRAME_DIRECTIONS,
3	  IDENTIFIER_LOGIC_OPERATORS,

5	  type FrameAsset,
6	  type ValidationIssue,
7	  type ValidationResult,
8	} from './types';
9	import { validateFrameField } from './validation-field';
10	
11	function createIssue(
12	  code: string,
13	  path: string,
14	  message: string,
15	  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',
16	): ValidationIssue {
17	  return { severity, code, path, message };
18	}
19	
20	function toResult(issues: ValidationIssue[]): ValidationResult {
21	  return {
22	    valid: issues.every((issue) => issue.severity !== 'error'),
23	    issues,
24	  };
25	}
26	
27	function isOneOf<T extends readonly string[]>(value: string, options: T): value is T[number] {
28	  return (options as readonly string[]).includes(value);
29	}
30	
31	function hasText(value: string | undefined): boolean {
32	  return typeof value === 'string' && value.trim().length > 0;
33	}
34	
35	export function validateFrameAsset(frame: FrameAsset, path = 'frame'): ValidationResult {
36	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
37	
38	  if (!hasText(frame.id)) {
39	    issues.push(createIssue('frame.idMissing', `${path}.id`, '帧 ID 不能为空'));
40	  }
41	
42	  if (!hasText(frame.name)) {
43	    issues.push(createIssue('frame.nameMissing', `${path}.name`, '帧名称不能为空'));
44	  }
45	
46	  if (!isOneOf(frame.direction, FRAME_DIRECTIONS)) {
47	    issues.push(createIssue('frame.directionUnsupported', `${path}.direction`, '帧方向必须是 send 或 receive'));
48	  }
49	
50	  if (!Array.isArray(frame.fields) || frame.fields.length === 0) {
51	    issues.push(createIssue('frame.fieldsMissing', `${path}.fields`, '帧至少需要一个字段'));
52	    return toResult(issues);
53	  }
54	
55	  const names = new Map<string, number>();
56	  for (const [index, field] of frame.fields.entries()) {
57	    issues.push(...validateFrameField(field, `${path}.fields.${index}`).issues);
58	
59	    const fieldName = field.name.trim();
60	    if (!fieldName) {
61	      continue;
62	    }
63	
64	    const count = names.get(fieldName) ?? 0;
65	    names.set(fieldName, count + 1);
66	  }
67	
68	  for (const [name, count] of names.entries()) {
69	    if (count > 1) {
70	      issues.push(createIssue('frame.fieldNameDuplicate', `${path}.fields`, `字段名称必须唯一: ${name}`));
71	    }
72	  }
73	
74	  if (frame.identifierRules) {
75	    for (const [index, rule] of frame.identifierRules.entries()) {
76	      const rulePath = `${path}.identifierRules.${index}`;
77	      if (!Number.isInteger(rule.startIndex) || rule.startIndex < 0) {
78	        issues.push(createIssue('frame.identifierRuleStartIndex', `${rulePath}.startIndex`, '标识规则 startIndex 必须是非负整数'));
79	      }
80	      if (!Number.isInteger(rule.endIndex) || rule.endIndex < 0) {
81	        issues.push(createIssue('frame.identifierRuleEndIndex', `${rulePath}.endIndex`, '标识规则 endIndex 必须是非负整数'));
82	      }
83	      if (!isOneOf(rule.operator, IDENTIFIER_RULE_OPERATORS)) {
84	        issues.push(createIssue('frame.identifierRuleOperator', `${rulePath}.operator`, '标识规则 operator 不在枚举内'));
85	      }
86	      if (!isOneOf(rule.logicOperator, IDENTIFIER_LOGIC_OPERATORS)) {
87	        issues.push(createIssue('frame.identifierRuleLogicOperator', `${rulePath}.logicOperator`, '标识规则 logicOperator 不在枚举内'));
88	      }
89	    }
90	  }
91	
92	  return toResult(issues);
93	}
94	
95	export function validateFrameAssetCollection(frames: readonly FrameAsset[]): ValidationResult {
96	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
97	  const ids = new Map<string, number>();
98	
99	  for (const [index, frame] of frames.entries()) {
100	    issues.push(...validateFrameAsset(frame, `frames.${index}`).issues);
101	
102	    const id = frame.id.trim();
103	    if (!id) {
104	      continue;
105	    }
106	
107	    ids.set(id, (ids.get(id) ?? 0) + 1);
108	  }
109	
110	  for (const [id, count] of ids.entries()) {
111	    if (count > 1) {
112	      issues.push(createIssue('frame.idDuplicate', 'frames', `帧 ID 必须唯一: ${id}`));
113	    }
114	  }
115	
116	  return toResult(issues);
117	}
118

1	import { normalizeFrame, isRecordCheck, createIssue } from './legacy-normalizers';
2	import { validateFrameAssetCollection } from './validation-frame';
3	import type { FrameAsset, LegacyFrameMigrationResult, ValidationIssue } from './types';
4	
5	export function isLegacyFrameConfigJson(value: unknown): boolean {
6	  return Array.isArray(value) && value.every((item) => isRecordCheck(item) && Array.isArray(item.fields));
7	}
8	
9	export function migrateLegacyFrameConfig(value: unknown): LegacyFrameMigrationResult {
10	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
11	
12	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
13	    return {
14	      recognized: false,
15	      frames: [],
16	      issues: [createIssue('legacy.rootInvalid', 'legacy', '旧 frame 导入内容必须是数组')],
17	    };
18	  }
19	
20	  const frames = value
21	    .map((item, index) => normalizeFrame(item, index, issues))
22	    .filter((frame): frame is FrameAsset => frame !== undefined);
23	
24	  const validation = validateFrameAssetCollection(frames);
25	
26	  return {
27	    recognized: true,
28	    frames,
29	    issues: [...issues, ...validation.issues],
30	  };
31	}
32

1	import {
2	  CHECKSUM_METHODS,
3	  DATA_PARTICIPATION_TYPES,
4	  FRAME_DATA_TYPES,
5	  FRAME_DIRECTIONS,
6	  FRAME_INPUT_TYPES,
7	  IDENTIFIER_LOGIC_OPERATORS,
8	  IDENTIFIER_RULE_OPERATORS,
9	  type ChecksumMethod,
10	  type DataParticipationType,
11	  type ExpressionDefinition,
12	  type ExpressionVariableDefinition,
13	  type FrameAsset,
14	  type FrameDataType,
15	  type FrameDirection,
16	  type FrameFieldDefinition,
17	  type FrameInputType,
18	  type FrameOptionDefinition,
19	  type IdentifierRule,
20	  type ValidationIssue,
21	} from './types';
22	
23	type UnknownRecord = Record<string, unknown>;
24	
25	function issue(
26	  code: string,
27	  path: string,
28	  message: string,
29	  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',
30	): ValidationIssue {
31	  return { code, path, message, severity };
32	}
33	
34	function isRecord(value: unknown): value is UnknownRecord {
35	  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
36	}
37	
38	function stringValue(value: unknown, fallback = ''): string {
39	  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : fallback;
40	}
41	
42	function booleanValue(value: unknown, fallback: boolean): boolean {
43	  return typeof value === 'boolean' ? value : fallback;
44	}
45	
46	function isStringOption<T extends readonly string[]>(value: unknown, options: T): value is T[number] {
47	  return typeof value === 'string' && (options as readonly string[]).includes(value);
48	}
49	
50	function unknownValueLabel(value: unknown): string {
51	  if (typeof value === 'string' && value.length > 0) {
52	    return value;
53	  }
54	
55	  if (value === undefined) {
56	    return 'undefined';
57	  }
58	
59	  if (value === null) {
60	    return 'null';
61	  }
62	
63	  return String(value);
64	}
65	
66	function numberValue(value: unknown, fallback: number): number {
67	  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : fallback;
68	}
69	
70	function coerceToNumber(value: unknown, fallback: number): number {
71	  if (typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value)) return value;
72	  if (typeof value === 'string') {
73	    const n = Number(value);
74	    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
75	  }
76	  return fallback;
77	}
78	
79	function normalizeDirection(
80	  value: unknown,
81	  path: string,
82	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
83	): FrameDirection {
84	  if (isStringOption(value, FRAME_DIRECTIONS)) {
85	    return value;
86	  }
87	
88	  issues.push(
89	    issue(
90	      'legacy.directionUnsupported',
91	      path,
92	      `旧 frame direction 不受支持: ${unknownValueLabel(value)}`,
93	    ),
94	  );
95	  return stringValue(value) as FrameDirection;
96	}
97	
98	function normalizeDataType(
99	  value: unknown,
100	  path: string,
101	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
102	): FrameDataType {
103	  if (isStringOption(value, FRAME_DATA_TYPES)) {
104	    return value;
105	  }
106	
107	  issues.push(
108	    issue(
109	      'legacy.dataTypeUnsupported',
110	      path,
111	      `旧 field dataType 不受支持: ${unknownValueLabel(value)}`,
112	    ),
113	  );
114	  return stringValue(value) as FrameDataType;
115	}
116	
117	function normalizeInputType(
118	  value: unknown,
119	  path: string,
120	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
121	): FrameInputType {
122	  if (isStringOption(value, FRAME_INPUT_TYPES)) {
123	    return value;
124	  }
125	
126	  issues.push(
127	    issue(
128	      'legacy.inputTypeUnsupported',
129	      path,
130	      `旧 field inputType 不受支持: ${unknownValueLabel(value)}`,
131	    ),
132	  );
133	  return stringValue(value) as FrameInputType;
134	}
135	
136	function normalizeDataParticipationType(
137	  value: unknown,
138	  path: string,
139	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
140	): DataParticipationType {
141	  if (value === undefined) {
142	    issues.push(
143	      issue(
144	        'legacy.dataParticipationDefaulted',
145	        path,
146	        '旧 field 缺少 dataParticipationType，兼容迁移为 direct',
147	        'warning',
148	      ),
149	    );
150	    return 'direct';
151	  }
152	
153	  if (isStringOption(value, DATA_PARTICIPATION_TYPES)) {
154	    return value;
155	  }
156	
157	  issues.push(
158	    issue(
159	      'legacy.dataParticipationUnsupported',
160	      path,
161	      `旧 field dataParticipationType 不受支持: ${unknownValueLabel(value)}`,
162	    ),
163	  );
164	  return stringValue(value) as DataParticipationType;
165	}
166	
167	function normalizeOptions(value: unknown): FrameOptionDefinition[] {
168	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
169	    return [];
170	  }
171	
172	  return value.filter(isRecord).map((option) => ({
173	    value: stringValue(option.value),
174	    label: stringValue(option.label),
175	    ...(typeof option.isDefault === 'boolean' ? { isDefault: option.isDefault } : {}),
176	  }));
177	}
178	
179	function normalizeIdentifierRule(
180	  value: unknown,
181	  path: string,
182	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
183	): IdentifierRule | undefined {
184	  if (!isRecord(value)) {
185	    issues.push(issue('legacy.identifierRuleInvalid', path, '旧标识规则条目必须是对象'));
186	    return undefined;
187	  }
188	
189	  const operatorRaw = stringValue(value.operator);
190	  const operator = isStringOption(operatorRaw, IDENTIFIER_RULE_OPERATORS) ? operatorRaw : undefined;
191	  if (operatorRaw && !operator) {
192	    issues.push(
193	      issue('legacy.identifierRuleOperatorUnsupported', `${path}.operator`, `旧标识规则 operator 不在枚举内: ${operatorRaw}`, 'warning'),
194	    );
195	  }
196	
197	  const logicOperatorRaw = stringValue(value.logicOperator);
198	  const logicOperator = isStringOption(logicOperatorRaw, IDENTIFIER_LOGIC_OPERATORS) ? logicOperatorRaw : undefined;
199	  if (logicOperatorRaw && !logicOperator) {
200	    issues.push(
201	      issue('legacy.identifierRuleLogicOperatorUnsupported', `${path}.logicOperator`, `旧标识规则 logicOperator 不在枚举内: ${logicOperatorRaw}`, 'warning'),
202	    );
203	  }
204	
205	  return {
206	    startIndex: coerceToNumber(value.startIndex, 0),
207	    endIndex: coerceToNumber(value.endIndex, 0),
208	    operator: operator ?? 'eq',
209	    value: stringValue(value.value),
210	    logicOperator: logicOperator ?? 'and',
211	  };
212	}
213	
214	function normalizeExpression(value: unknown): ExpressionDefinition | undefined {
215	  if (!isRecord(value)) {
216	    return undefined;
217	  }
218	
219	  const expressions = Array.isArray(value.expressions)
220	    ? value.expressions.filter(isRecord).map((expression) => ({
221	        condition: stringValue(expression.condition),
222	        expression: stringValue(expression.expression),
223	      }))
224	    : [];
225	
226	  const variables: ExpressionVariableDefinition[] = Array.isArray(value.variables)
227	    ? value.variables.filter(isRecord).map((variable) => ({
228	        identifier: stringValue(variable.identifier),
229	        sourceType: stringValue(variable.sourceType),
230	        sourceId: stringValue(variable.sourceId),
231	        frameId: stringValue(variable.frameId),
232	        fieldId: stringValue(variable.fieldId),
233	      }))
234	    : [];
235	
236	  return { expressions, variables };
237	}
238	
239	function normalizeField(
240	  value: unknown,
241	  frameIndex: number,
242	  index: number,
243	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
244	): FrameFieldDefinition | undefined {
245	  if (!isRecord(value)) {
246	    issues.push(issue('legacy.fieldInvalid', `legacy.${frameIndex}.fields.${index}`, '旧 field 条目必须是对象'));
247	    return undefined;
248	  }
249	
250	  const fieldPath = `legacy.${frameIndex}.fields.${index}`;
251	  const dataType = normalizeDataType(value.dataType, `${fieldPath}.dataType`, issues);
252	  const inputType = normalizeInputType(value.inputType, `${fieldPath}.inputType`, issues);
253	  const dataParticipationType = normalizeDataParticipationType(
254	    value.dataParticipationType,
255	    `${fieldPath}.dataParticipationType`,
256	    issues,
257	  );
258	
259	  const field: FrameFieldDefinition = {
260	    id: stringValue(value.id, `legacy-field-${index + 1}`),
261	    name: stringValue(value.name),
262	    dataType,
263	    length: numberValue(value.length, dataType === 'bytes' ? 1 : 0),
264	    description: stringValue(value.description),
265	    defaultValue: stringValue(value.defaultValue),
266	    inputType,
267	    configurable: booleanValue(value.configurable, false),
268	    options: normalizeOptions(value.options),
269	    dataParticipationType,
270	  };
271	
272	  if (isRecord(value.validOption)) {
273	    const checksumMethodRaw = stringValue(value.validOption.checksumMethod);
274	    const checksumMethod: ChecksumMethod | undefined = isStringOption(checksumMethodRaw, CHECKSUM_METHODS)
275	      ? checksumMethodRaw
276	      : undefined;
277	    if (checksumMethodRaw && !checksumMethod) {
278	      issues.push(
279	        issue(
280	          'legacy.checksumMethodUnsupported',
281	          `${fieldPath}.validOption.checksumMethod`,
282	          `旧 checksumMethod 不在枚举内: ${checksumMethodRaw}`,
283	          'warning',
284	        ),
285	      );
286	    }
287	
288	    field.validOption = {
289	      isChecksum: booleanValue(value.validOption.isChecksum, false),
290	      startFieldIndex: coerceToNumber(value.validOption.startFieldIndex, 0),
291	      endFieldIndex: coerceToNumber(value.validOption.endFieldIndex, 0),
292	      ...(checksumMethod ? { checksumMethod } : {}),
293	    };
294	  }
295	
296	  if (inputType === 'expression') {
297	    const expressionConfig = normalizeExpression(value.expressionConfig);
298	    if (expressionConfig) {
299	      field.expressionConfig = expressionConfig;
300	    }
301	  }
302	
303	  if (typeof value.bigEndian === 'boolean') {
304	    field.bigEndian = value.bigEndian;
305	  }
306	
307	  if (typeof value.isASCII === 'boolean') {
308	    field.isASCII = value.isASCII;
309	  }
310	
311	  if (value.factor !== undefined) {
312	    field.factor = coerceToNumber(value.factor, 1);
313	  }
314	
315	  return field;
316	}
317	
318	export function normalizeFrame(value: unknown, index: number, issues: ValidationIssue[]): FrameAsset | undefined {
319	  if (!isRecord(value)) {
320	    issues.push(issue('legacy.frameInvalid', `legacy.${index}`, '旧 frame 条目必须是对象'));
321	    return undefined;
322	  }
323	
324	  const rawFields = Array.isArray(value.fields) ? value.fields : [];
325	  if (!Array.isArray(value.fields)) {
326	    issues.push(issue('legacy.fieldsInvalid', `legacy.${index}.fields`, '旧 frame 缺少 fields 数组'));
327	  }
328	
329	  const direction = normalizeDirection(value.direction, `legacy.${index}.direction`, issues);
330	
331	  const frame: FrameAsset = {
332	    id: stringValue(value.id, `legacy-frame-${index + 1}`),
333	    name: stringValue(value.name),
334	    direction,
335	    fields: rawFields
336	      .map((field, fieldIndex) => normalizeField(field, index, fieldIndex, issues))
337	      .filter((field): field is FrameFieldDefinition => field !== undefined),
338	    description: stringValue(value.description),
339	    frameType: stringValue(value.frameType),
340	    protocol: stringValue(value.protocol),
341	    isFavorite: booleanValue(value.isFavorite, false),
342	    identifierRules: Array.isArray(value.identifierRules)
343	      ? value.identifierRules
344	          .map((rule, ruleIndex) => normalizeIdentifierRule(rule, `legacy.${index}.identifierRules.${ruleIndex}`, issues))
345	          .filter((rule): rule is IdentifierRule => rule !== undefined)
346	      : [],
347	  };
348	
349	  if (isRecord(value.options)) {
350	    frame.options = {
351	      autoChecksum: booleanValue(value.options.autoChecksum, true),
352	      bigEndian: booleanValue(value.options.bigEndian, false),
353	      includeLengthField: booleanValue(value.options.includeLengthField, false),
354	    };
355	  }
356	
357	  if (typeof value.createdAt === 'string') {
358	    frame.createdAt = value.createdAt;
359	  }
360	
361	  if (typeof value.updatedAt === 'string') {
362	    frame.updatedAt = value.updatedAt;
363	  }
364	
365	  return frame;
366	}
367	
368	export function isRecordCheck(value: unknown): value is UnknownRecord {
369	  return isRecord(value);
370	}
371	
372	export function createIssue(
373	  code: string,
374	  path: string,
375	  message: string,
376	  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',
377	): ValidationIssue {
378	  return issue(code, path, message, severity);
379	}
380

1	import {
2	  migrateLegacyFrameConfig,
3	  validateFrameAssetCollection,
4	  type FrameAsset,
5	  type LegacyFrameMigrationResult,
6	  type ReadonlyFrameAsset,
7	  type ValidationIssue,
8	  type ValidationResult,
9	} from '../core';
10	import {
11	  findFrameAssets,
12	  getFrameAsset,
13	  getSelectedFrameAsset,
14	  listFrameAssetSummaries,
15	  selectFieldReferenceOptions,
16	  selectFrameReferenceOptions,
17	  type FrameAssetQuery,
18	  type FrameAssetSnapshot,
19	  type FrameAssetSummary,
20	  type FrameFieldReference,
21	  type FrameFieldReferenceQuery,
22	  type FrameReferenceOption,
23	} from '../selectors';
24	import { createFrameState, type FrameStateContainer, type FrameStateSnapshot } from '../state';
25	
26	export interface FrameAssetReader {
27	  getSnapshot(): FrameAssetSnapshot;
28	  listFrames(query?: FrameAssetQuery): FrameAssetSummary[];
29	  findFrames(query?: FrameAssetQuery): ReadonlyFrameAsset[];
30	  getFrame(frameId: string): ReadonlyFrameAsset | undefined;
31	  getSelectedFrame(): ReadonlyFrameAsset | undefined;
32	  listFrameReferences(query?: FrameAssetQuery): FrameReferenceOption[];
33	  listFieldReferences(query?: FrameFieldReferenceQuery): FrameFieldReference[];
34	}
35	
36	export interface FrameAssetOperationResult {
37	  readonly ok: boolean;
38	  readonly validation: ValidationResult;
39	  readonly snapshot: FrameStateSnapshot;
40	}
41	
42	export interface FrameLegacyLoadResult extends FrameAssetOperationResult {
43	  readonly migration: LegacyFrameMigrationResult;
44	}
45	
46	export interface FrameAssetService extends FrameAssetReader {
47	  replaceFrames(frames: readonly FrameAsset[], selectedFrameId?: string): FrameAssetOperationResult;
48	  loadLegacyFrameConfig(value: unknown, selectedFrameId?: string): FrameLegacyLoadResult;
49	  selectFrame(frameId: string | undefined): FrameAssetOperationResult;
50	}
51	
52	function hasError(issues: readonly ValidationIssue[]): boolean {
53	  return issues.some((issue) => issue.severity === 'error');
54	}
55	
56	function validResult(): ValidationResult {
57	  return {
58	    valid: true,
59	    issues: [],
60	  };
61	}
62	
63	function issue(
64	  code: string,
65	  path: string,
66	  message: string,
67	  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',
68	): ValidationIssue {
69	  return { code, path, message, severity };
70	}
71	
72	function invalidResult(issues: ValidationIssue[]): ValidationResult {
73	  return {
74	    valid: false,
75	    issues,
76	  };
77	}
78	
79	export function createFrameAssetReader(snapshotProvider: () => FrameAssetSnapshot): FrameAssetReader {
80	  return {
81	    getSnapshot() {
82	      const snapshot = snapshotProvider();
83	      return {
84	        frames: findFrameAssets(snapshot),
85	        ...(snapshot.selectedFrameId ? { selectedFrameId: snapshot.selectedFrameId } : {}),
86	      };
87	    },
88	
89	    listFrames(query) {
90	      return listFrameAssetSummaries(snapshotProvider(), query);
91	    },
92	
93	    findFrames(query) {
94	      return findFrameAssets(snapshotProvider(), query);
95	    },
96	
97	    getFrame(frameId) {
98	      return getFrameAsset(snapshotProvider(), frameId);
99	    },
100	
101	    getSelectedFrame() {
102	      return getSelectedFrameAsset(snapshotProvider());
103	    },
104	
105	    listFrameReferences(query) {
106	      return selectFrameReferenceOptions(snapshotProvider(), query);
107	    },
108	
109	    listFieldReferences(query) {
110	      return selectFieldReferenceOptions(snapshotProvider(), query);
111	    },
112	  };
113	}
114	
115	export function createFrameAssetService(
116	  state: FrameStateContainer = createFrameState(),
117	): FrameAssetService {
118	  const reader = createFrameAssetReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
119	
120	  return {
121	    ...reader,
122	
123	    replaceFrames(frames, selectedFrameId) {
124	      const validation = validateFrameAssetCollection(frames);
125	      if (!validation.valid) {
126	        return {
127	          ok: false,
128	          validation,
129	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
130	        };
131	      }
132	
133	      return {
134	        ok: true,
135	        validation,
136	        snapshot: state.replaceFrames(frames, selectedFrameId),
137	      };
138	    },
139	
140	    loadLegacyFrameConfig(value, selectedFrameId) {
141	      const migration = migrateLegacyFrameConfig(value);
142	      if (!migration.recognized || hasError(migration.issues)) {
143	        return {
144	          ok: false,
145	          validation: {
146	            valid: !hasError(migration.issues),
147	            issues: migration.issues,
148	          },
149	          migration,
150	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
151	        };
152	      }
153	
154	      return {
155	        ok: true,
156	        validation: {
157	          valid: true,
158	          issues: migration.issues,
159	        },
160	        migration,
161	        snapshot: state.replaceFrames(migration.frames, selectedFrameId),
162	      };
163	    },
164	
165	    selectFrame(frameId) {
166	      if (frameId === undefined) {
167	        return {
168	          ok: true,
169	          validation: validResult(),
170	          snapshot: state.selectFrame(undefined),
171	        };
172	      }
173	
174	      if (!getFrameAsset(state.getSnapshot(), frameId)) {
175	        return {
176	          ok: false,
177	          validation: invalidResult([
178	            issue('frame.selectionMissing', 'selectedFrameId', `未找到帧: ${frameId}`),
179	          ]),
180	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
181	        };
182	      }
183	
184	      return {
185	        ok: true,
186	        validation: validResult(),
187	        snapshot: state.selectFrame(frameId),
188	      };
189	    },
190	  };
191	}
192	
193	export interface FrameAssetFile {
194	  schemaVersion: 1;
195	  frames: FrameAsset[];
196	}
197	
198	export interface FrameDeserializeResult {
199	  ok: boolean;
200	  frames: FrameAsset[];
201	  issues: ValidationIssue[];
202	}
203	
204	export function serializeFrames(frames: readonly FrameAsset[]): string {
205	  const file: FrameAssetFile = { schemaVersion: 1, frames: [...frames] };
206	  return JSON.stringify(file, null, 2);
207	}
208	
209	export function deserializeFrames(text: string): FrameDeserializeResult {
210	  let parsed: unknown;
211	  try {
212	    parsed = JSON.parse(text);
213	  } catch {
214	    // Try legacy path: the text might be a raw legacy array
215	    return tryLegacyDeserialize();
216	  }
217	
218	  if (isFrameAssetFile(parsed)) {
219	    const validation = validateFrameAssetCollection(parsed.frames);
220	    return {
221	      ok: !hasError(validation.issues),
222	      frames: parsed.frames,
223	      issues: validation.issues,
224	    };
225	  }
226	
227	  // No schemaVersion → legacy array or object
228	  const legacy = migrateLegacyFrameConfig(parsed);
229	  return {
230	    ok: legacy.recognized && !hasError(legacy.issues),
231	    frames: legacy.frames,
232	    issues: legacy.issues,
233	  };
234	}
235	
236	function isFrameAssetFile(value: unknown): value is FrameAssetFile {
237	  return (
238	    typeof value === 'object' &&
239	    value !== null &&
240	    'schemaVersion' in value &&
241	    (value as FrameAssetFile).schemaVersion === 1 &&
242	    'frames' in value &&
243	    Array.isArray((value as FrameAssetFile).frames)
244	  );
245	}
246	
247	function tryLegacyDeserialize(): FrameDeserializeResult {
248	  return {
249	    ok: false,
250	    frames: [],
251	    issues: [issue('deserialize.parseError', 'input', 'JSON 格式错误，无法解析')],
252	  };
253	}
254

1	import {
2	  cloneFrameAsset,
3	  cloneFrameAssets,
4	  type FrameAsset,
5	  type ReadonlyFrameAsset,
6	} from '../core';
7	
8	export interface FrameStateSnapshot {
9	  readonly frames: readonly ReadonlyFrameAsset[];
10	  readonly selectedFrameId?: string;
11	}
12	
13	export interface FrameStateInitialValue {
14	  readonly frames?: readonly FrameAsset[];
15	  readonly selectedFrameId?: string;
16	}
17	
18	export interface FrameStateContainer {
19	  getSnapshot(): FrameStateSnapshot;
20	  replaceFrames(frames: readonly FrameAsset[], selectedFrameId?: string): FrameStateSnapshot;
21	  upsertFrame(frame: FrameAsset): FrameStateSnapshot;
22	  removeFrame(frameId: string): FrameStateSnapshot;
23	  selectFrame(frameId: string | undefined): FrameStateSnapshot;
24	}
25	
26	function hasFrame(frames: readonly FrameAsset[], frameId: string | undefined): boolean {
27	  return typeof frameId === 'string' && frames.some((frame) => frame.id === frameId);
28	}
29	
30	function createSnapshot(
31	  frames: readonly FrameAsset[],
32	  selectedFrameId: string | undefined,
33	): FrameStateSnapshot {
34	  return {
35	    frames: cloneFrameAssets(frames),
36	    ...(selectedFrameId ? { selectedFrameId } : {}),
37	  };
38	}
39	
40	export function createFrameState(initialValue: FrameStateInitialValue = {}): FrameStateContainer {
41	  let frames = cloneFrameAssets(initialValue.frames ?? []);
42	  let selectedFrameId = hasFrame(frames, initialValue.selectedFrameId)
43	    ? initialValue.selectedFrameId
44	    : undefined;
45	
46	  return {
47	    getSnapshot() {
48	      return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
49	    },
50	
51	    replaceFrames(nextFrames, nextSelectedFrameId = selectedFrameId) {
52	      frames = cloneFrameAssets(nextFrames);
53	      selectedFrameId = hasFrame(frames, nextSelectedFrameId) ? nextSelectedFrameId : undefined;
54	      return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
55	    },
56	
57	    upsertFrame(frame) {
58	      const nextFrame = cloneFrameAsset(frame);
59	      const index = frames.findIndex((item) => item.id === frame.id);
60	
61	      if (index === -1) {
62	        frames = [...frames, nextFrame];
63	      } else {
64	        frames = frames.map((item, itemIndex) => (itemIndex === index ? nextFrame : item));
65	      }
66	
67	      return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
68	    },
69	
70	    removeFrame(frameId) {
71	      frames = frames.filter((frame) => frame.id !== frameId);
72	      if (selectedFrameId === frameId) {
73	        selectedFrameId = undefined;
74	      }
75	
76	      return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
77	    },
78	
79	    selectFrame(frameId) {
80	      selectedFrameId = hasFrame(frames, frameId) ? frameId : undefined;
81	      return createSnapshot(frames, selectedFrameId);
82	    },
83	  };
84	}
85

1	import type {
2	  ConnectionCounterSnapshot,
3	  ConnectionRuntimeFact,
4	  ConnectionStateSnapshot,
5	  ReadonlyConnectionRuntimeFact,
6	  ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
7	  ReadonlyTransportConfig,
8	  ReadonlyTransportEventSnapshot,

10	  TransportConfig,
11	  TransportErrorSnapshot,
12	  TransportEventSnapshot,
13	  TransportTargetSnapshot,
14	} from './types';
15	
16	export function cloneTransportConfig(config: ReadonlyTransportConfig): TransportConfig {
17	  switch (config.kind) {
18	    case 'serial':
19	      return {
20	        id: config.id,
21	        kind: config.kind,
22	        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),
23	        portPath: config.portPath,
24	        baudRate: config.baudRate,
25	      };
26	    case 'tcp-client':
27	      return {
28	        id: config.id,
29	        kind: config.kind,
30	        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),
31	        host: config.host,
32	        port: config.port,
33	      };
34	    case 'tcp-server':
35	      return {
36	        id: config.id,
37	        kind: config.kind,
38	        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),
39	        host: config.host,
40	        port: config.port,
41	      };
42	    case 'udp':
43	      return {
44	        id: config.id,
45	        kind: config.kind,
46	        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),
47	        localHost: config.localHost,
48	        localPort: config.localPort,
49	        ...(config.remoteHost ? { remoteHost: config.remoteHost } : {}),
50	        ...(config.remotePort !== undefined ? { remotePort: config.remotePort } : {}),
51	      };
52	  }
53	}
54	
55	export function cloneTransportConfigs(
56	  configs: readonly ReadonlyTransportConfig[],
57	): TransportConfig[] {
58	  return configs.map(cloneTransportConfig);
59	}
60	
61	export function cloneConnectionCounters(
62	  counters: Readonly<ConnectionCounterSnapshot>,
63	): ConnectionCounterSnapshot {
64	  return { ...counters };
65	}
66	
67	export function cloneTransportTarget(
68	  target: ReadonlyTransportTargetSnapshot,
69	): TransportTargetSnapshot {
70	  return { ...target };
71	}
72	
73	export function cloneTransportError(error: Readonly<TransportErrorSnapshot>): TransportErrorSnapshot {
74	  return {
75	    kind: error.kind,
76	    message: error.message,
77	    occurredAt: error.occurredAt,
78	    ...(error.connectionId ? { connectionId: error.connectionId } : {}),
79	    ...(error.recoverable !== undefined ? { recoverable: error.recoverable } : {}),
80	  };
81	}
82	
83	export function cloneTransportEvent(
84	  event: ReadonlyTransportEventSnapshot,
85	): TransportEventSnapshot {
86	  return {
87	    id: event.id,
88	    kind: event.kind,
89	    connectionId: event.connectionId,
90	    occurredAt: event.occurredAt,
91	    ...(event.target ? { target: cloneTransportTarget(event.target) } : {}),
92	    ...(event.byteLength !== undefined ? { byteLength: event.byteLength } : {}),
93	    ...(event.error ? { error: cloneTransportError(event.error) } : {}),
94	    ...(event.reconnectAttempt !== undefined ? { reconnectAttempt: event.reconnectAttempt } : {}),
95	    ...(event.reconnectNextAt ? { reconnectNextAt: event.reconnectNextAt } : {}),
96	  };
97	}
98	
99	export function cloneConnectionRuntimeFact(
100	  fact: ReadonlyConnectionRuntimeFact,
101	): ConnectionRuntimeFact {
102	  return {
103	    connectionId: fact.connectionId,
104	    kind: fact.kind,
105	    lifecycle: fact.lifecycle,
106	    config: cloneTransportConfig(fact.config),
107	    target: cloneTransportTarget(fact.target),
108	    counters: cloneConnectionCounters(fact.counters),
109	    ...(fact.lastActivityAt ? { lastActivityAt: fact.lastActivityAt } : {}),
110	    ...(fact.lastError ? { lastError: cloneTransportError(fact.lastError) } : {}),
111	    ...(fact.reconnectAttempt !== undefined ? { reconnectAttempt: fact.reconnectAttempt } : {}),
112	    ...(fact.reconnectNextAt ? { reconnectNextAt: fact.reconnectNextAt } : {}),
113	  };
114	}
115	
116	export function cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(
117	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
118	): ConnectionStateSnapshot {
119	  return {
120	    schemaVersion: snapshot.schemaVersion,
121	    configs: cloneTransportConfigs(snapshot.configs),
122	    runtimeFacts: snapshot.runtimeFacts.map(cloneConnectionRuntimeFact),
123	    events: snapshot.events.map(cloneTransportEvent),
124	    ...(snapshot.lastError ? { lastError: cloneTransportError(snapshot.lastError) } : {}),
125	  };
126	}

1	import {
2	  cloneConnectionRuntimeFact,
3	  cloneConnectionStateSnapshot,
4	  cloneTransportConfig,
5	  cloneTransportError,
6	  cloneTransportEvent,
7	  cloneTransportTarget,
8	  type ConnectionRuntimeFact,
9	  type ConnectionStateSnapshot,
10	  type ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
11	  type TransportConfig,
12	  type TransportErrorSnapshot,
13	  type TransportEventSnapshot,
14	  type TransportKind,
15	  type TransportTargetSnapshot,
16	} from '../core';
17	import { getReconnectPolicy, type ReconnectPolicy } from '../core/reconnect';
18	
19	export interface ConnectionTargetQuery {
20	  readonly kind?: TransportKind;
21	  readonly availableOnly?: boolean;
22	}
23	
24	export interface ConnectionSummary {
25	  readonly connectionId: string;
26	  readonly kind: TransportKind;
27	  readonly lifecycle: ConnectionRuntimeFact['lifecycle'];
28	  readonly label: string;
29	  readonly routeLabel: string;
30	  readonly available: boolean;
31	  readonly rxBytes: number;
32	  readonly txBytes: number;
33	  readonly errorCount: number;
34	  readonly lastActivityAt?: string;
35	  readonly lastError?: TransportErrorSnapshot;
36	}
37	
38	function matchesTarget(
39	  target: TransportTargetSnapshot,
40	  query: ConnectionTargetQuery | undefined,
41	): boolean {
42	  if (query?.kind && target.kind !== query.kind) {
43	    return false;
44	  }
45	
46	  return query?.availableOnly ? target.available : true;
47	}
48	
49	function toSummary(fact: ConnectionRuntimeFact): ConnectionSummary {
50	  return {
51	    connectionId: fact.connectionId,
52	    kind: fact.kind,
53	    lifecycle: fact.lifecycle,
54	    label: fact.target.label,
55	    routeLabel: fact.target.routeLabel,
56	    available: fact.target.available,
57	    rxBytes: fact.counters.rxBytes,
58	    txBytes: fact.counters.txBytes,
59	    errorCount: fact.counters.errorCount,
60	    ...(fact.lastActivityAt ? { lastActivityAt: fact.lastActivityAt } : {}),
61	    ...(fact.lastError ? { lastError: cloneTransportError(fact.lastError) } : {}),
62	  };
63	}
64	
65	export function selectConnectionSnapshot(
66	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
67	): ConnectionStateSnapshot {
68	  return cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(snapshot);
69	}
70	
71	export function selectTransportConfigs(
72	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
73	): TransportConfig[] {
74	  return snapshot.configs.map(cloneTransportConfig);
75	}
76	
77	export function selectConnectionFacts(
78	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
79	): ConnectionRuntimeFact[] {
80	  return snapshot.runtimeFacts.map(cloneConnectionRuntimeFact);
81	}
82	
83	export function selectConnectionFact(
84	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
85	  connectionId: string,
86	): ConnectionRuntimeFact | undefined {
87	  const fact = snapshot.runtimeFacts.find((item) => item.connectionId === connectionId);
88	  return fact ? cloneConnectionRuntimeFact(fact) : undefined;
89	}
90	
91	export function selectConnectionSummaries(
92	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
93	): ConnectionSummary[] {
94	  return snapshot.runtimeFacts.map(toSummary);
95	}
96	
97	export function selectTransportTargets(
98	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
99	  query?: ConnectionTargetQuery,
100	): TransportTargetSnapshot[] {
101	  return snapshot.runtimeFacts
102	    .map((fact) => fact.target)
103	    .filter((target) => matchesTarget(target, query))
104	    .map(cloneTransportTarget);
105	}
106	
107	export function selectLastTransportError(
108	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
109	): TransportErrorSnapshot | undefined {
110	  return snapshot.lastError ? cloneTransportError(snapshot.lastError) : undefined;
111	}
112	
113	export function selectTransportEvents(
114	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
115	): TransportEventSnapshot[] {
116	  return snapshot.events.map(cloneTransportEvent);
117	}
118	
119	export interface ReconnectStatus {
120	  readonly isActive: boolean;
121	  readonly attempt: number;
122	  readonly nextAt?: string;
123	  readonly policy: ReconnectPolicy;
124	}
125	
126	export function selectReconnectStatus(
127	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
128	  connectionId: string,
129	): ReconnectStatus | undefined {
130	  const fact = snapshot.runtimeFacts.find((item) => item.connectionId === connectionId);
131	  if (!fact) return undefined;
132	
133	  const policy = getReconnectPolicy(fact.kind);
134	  const isActive = fact.reconnectAttempt !== undefined;
135	
136	  return {
137	    isActive,
138	    attempt: fact.reconnectAttempt ?? 0,
139	    ...(fact.reconnectNextAt ? { nextAt: fact.reconnectNextAt } : {}),
140	    policy,
141	  };
142	}
143	
```

3	  createConnectionIssue,

17	import { getReconnectPolicy, nextReconnectDelay, shouldReconnect } from '../core/reconnect';

21	  ConnectionAdapterEvent,
22	  ConnectionTransportAdapter,
23	  TransportWriteRequest,

25	import { createConnectionState, type ConnectionStateContainer } from '../state/connection-state';

39	export interface ConnectionReader {
40	  getSnapshot(): ConnectionStateSnapshot;
41	  listTransportConfigs(): TransportConfig[];
42	  listConnectionFacts(): ConnectionRuntimeFact[];
43	  getConnectionFact(connectionId: string): ConnectionRuntimeFact | undefined;
44	  listConnectionSummaries(): ConnectionSummary[];
45	  listTransportTargets(query?: ConnectionTargetQuery): TransportTargetSnapshot[];
46	  getLastTransportError(): TransportErrorSnapshot | undefined;
47	  listTransportEvents(): TransportEventSnapshot[];
48	}
49	
50	export interface ConnectionOperationOutcome {
51	  readonly ok: boolean;
52	  readonly validation: ConnectionValidationOutcome;
53	  readonly snapshot: ConnectionStateSnapshot;
54	  readonly events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[];
55	  readonly error?: TransportErrorSnapshot;
56	}
57	
58	export interface ConnectionService extends ConnectionReader {
59	  connect(config: unknown): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
60	  disconnect(connectionId: string): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
61	  write(request: TransportWriteRequest): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
62	  drainAdapterEvents(): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
63	  cleanup(): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
64	}
65	
66	export interface CreateConnectionServiceOptions {
67	  readonly adapter: ConnectionTransportAdapter;
68	  readonly state?: ConnectionStateContainer;
69	  readonly now?: () => string;
70	}
71	
72	
73	function validValidation(): ConnectionValidationOutcome {
74	  return createConnectionValidationOutcome([]);
75	}
76	
77	function invalidConfigValidation(): ConnectionValidationOutcome {
78	  return createConnectionValidationOutcome([
79	    createConnectionIssue(
80	      'connection.config.missing',
81	      'config',
82	      'Connection config could not be normalized.',
83	      'error',
84	    ),
85	  ]);
86	}
87	
88	function adapterErrorToSnapshot(
89	  error: ConnectionAdapterErrorInput,
90	  connectionId: string,
91	  occurredAt: string,
92	): TransportErrorSnapshot {
93	  return {
94	    kind: error.kind,
95	    message: error.message,
96	    occurredAt,
97	    connectionId,
98	    ...(error.recoverable !== undefined ? { recoverable: error.recoverable } : {}),
99	  };
100	}
101	
102	function adapterEventToNormalized(
103	  event: ConnectionAdapterEvent,
104	  now: () => string,
105	): NormalizedTransportEventInput {
106	  const occurredAt = event.occurredAt ?? now();
107	  return {
108	    kind: event.kind,
109	    connectionId: event.connectionId,
110	    occurredAt,
111	    ...(event.target ? { target: event.target } : {}),
112	    ...(event.byteLength !== undefined
113	      ? { byteLength: event.byteLength }
114	      : event.bytes
115	        ? { byteLength: event.bytes.length }
116	        : {}),
117	    ...(event.bytes ? { bytes: event.bytes } : {}),
118	    ...(event.error
119	      ? {
120	          error: {
121	            kind: event.error.kind,
122	            message: event.error.message,
123	            ...(event.error.recoverable !== undefined
124	              ? { recoverable: event.error.recoverable }
125	              : {}),
126	          },
127	        }
128	      : {}),
129	  };
130	}
131	
132	function toErrorEvent(
133	  connectionId: string,
134	  error: ConnectionAdapterErrorInput,
135	  occurredAt: string,
136	): NormalizedTransportEventInput {
137	  return {
138	    kind: 'error',
139	    connectionId,
140	    occurredAt,
141	    error,
142	  };
143	}
144	
145	function collectEventsAfter(
146	  beforeLength: number,
147	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
148	): TransportEventSnapshot[] {
149	  return snapshot.events.slice(beforeLength).map(cloneTransportEvent);
150	}
151	
152	export function createConnectionReader(
153	  snapshotProvider: () => ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
154	): ConnectionReader {
155	  return {
156	    getSnapshot() {
157	      return selectConnectionSnapshot(snapshotProvider());
158	    },
159	
160	    listTransportConfigs() {
161	      return selectTransportConfigs(snapshotProvider());
162	    },
163	
164	    listConnectionFacts() {
165	      return selectConnectionFacts(snapshotProvider());
166	    },
167	
168	    getConnectionFact(connectionId) {
169	      return selectConnectionFact(snapshotProvider(), connectionId);
170	    },
171	
172	    listConnectionSummaries() {
173	      return selectConnectionSummaries(snapshotProvider());
174	    },
175	
176	    listTransportTargets(query) {
177	      return selectTransportTargets(snapshotProvider(), query);
178	    },
179	
180	    getLastTransportError() {
181	      return selectLastTransportError(snapshotProvider());
182	    },
183	
184	    listTransportEvents() {
185	      return selectTransportEvents(snapshotProvider());
186	    },
187	  };
188	}
189	
190	interface ReconnectTimerEntry {
191	  timerId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;
192	  config: TransportConfig;
193	  attempt: number;
194	}
195	
196	export function createConnectionService(
197	  options: CreateConnectionServiceOptions,
198	): ConnectionService {
199	  const state = options.state ?? createConnectionState();
200	  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;
201	  const reader = createConnectionReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
202	  const reconnectTimers = new Map<string, ReconnectTimerEntry>();
203	
204	  function applyEvents(events: readonly ConnectionAdapterEvent[]): void {
205	    for (const event of events) {
206	      state.applyEvent(adapterEventToNormalized(event, now));
207	    }
208	  }
209	
210	  function clearReconnectTimer(connectionId: string): void {
211	    const entry = reconnectTimers.get(connectionId);
212	    if (entry) {
213	      clearTimeout(entry.timerId);
214	      reconnectTimers.delete(connectionId);
215	    }
216	  }
217	
218	  function clearAllReconnectTimers(): void {
219	    for (const entry of reconnectTimers.values()) {
220	      clearTimeout(entry.timerId);
221	    }
222	    reconnectTimers.clear();
223	  }
224	
225	  function resetReconnectState(connectionId: string): void {
226	    const fact = state.getSnapshot().runtimeFacts.find(
227	      (f) => f.connectionId === connectionId,
228	    );
229	    if (fact?.reconnectNextAt !== undefined) {
230	      state.applyEvent({
231	        kind: 'reconnect-exhausted',
232	        connectionId,
233	        occurredAt: now(),
234	      });
235	    }
236	  }
237	
238	  function scheduleReconnect(
239	    connectionId: string,
240	    config: TransportConfig,
241	    attempt: number,
242	    beforeEventsLength: number,
243	  ): TransportEventSnapshot[] {
244	    const policy = getReconnectPolicy(config.kind);
245	    const delay = nextReconnectDelay(policy, attempt);
246	    const nextAtTime = new Date(Date.now() + delay);
247	    const nextAt = nextAtTime.toISOString();
248	
249	    state.applyEvent({
250	      kind: 'reconnect-scheduled',
251	      connectionId,
252	      occurredAt: now(),
253	      reconnectAttempt: attempt,
254	      reconnectNextAt: nextAt,
255	    });
256	
257	    const timerId = setTimeout(() => {
258	      reconnectTimers.delete(connectionId);
259	      handleReconnectTimerFired(connectionId, config, attempt, policy);
260	    }, delay);
261	
262	    reconnectTimers.set(connectionId, { timerId, config, attempt });
263	
264	    const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
265	    return snapshot.events.slice(beforeEventsLength).map(cloneTransportEvent);
266	  }
267	
268	  async function handleReconnectTimerFired(
269	    connectionId: string,
270	    config: TransportConfig,
271	    attempt: number,
272	    policy: ReturnType<typeof getReconnectPolicy>,
273	  ): Promise<void> {
274	    try {
275	      const command = await options.adapter.connect(config);
276	      if (command.ok) {
277	        for (const event of command.events ?? []) {
278	          state.applyEvent(adapterEventToNormalized(event, now));
279	        }
280	        // connected event already resets reconnectAttempt/nextAt via reducer
281	      } else {
282	        // connect failed, increment attempt and try again
283	        for (const event of command.events ?? []) {
284	          state.applyEvent(adapterEventToNormalized(event, now));
285	        }
286	        if (command.events === undefined) {
287	          state.applyEvent({
288	            kind: 'error',
289	            connectionId,
290	            occurredAt: now(),
291	            error: command.error,
292	          });
293	        }
294	
295	        const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
296	        if (shouldReconnect(policy, nextAttempt)) {
297	          scheduleReconnect(connectionId, config, nextAttempt, state.getSnapshot().events.length);
298	        } else {
299	          state.applyEvent({
300	            kind: 'reconnect-exhausted',
301	            connectionId,
302	            occurredAt: now(),
303	          });
304	        }
305	      }
306	    } catch {
307	      const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;
308	      if (shouldReconnect(policy, nextAttempt)) {
309	        scheduleReconnect(connectionId, config, nextAttempt, state.getSnapshot().events.length);
310	      } else {
311	        state.applyEvent({
312	          kind: 'reconnect-exhausted',
313	          connectionId,
314	          occurredAt: now(),
315	        });
316	      }
317	    }
318	  }
319	
320	  function applyAdapterOutcome(
321	    connectionId: string,
322	    command: ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome,
323	  ): ConnectionOperationOutcome {
324	    const beforeLength = state.getSnapshot().events.length;
325	
326	    applyEvents(command.events ?? []);
327	    if (!command.ok && command.events === undefined) {
328	      state.applyEvent(toErrorEvent(connectionId, command.error, now()));
329	    }
330	
331	    const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
332	    const events = collectEventsAfter(beforeLength, snapshot);
333	    const lastEvent = events[events.length - 1];
334	    const error = !command.ok
335	      ? adapterErrorToSnapshot(command.error, connectionId, lastEvent?.occurredAt ?? now())
336	      : undefined;
337	
338	    return {
339	      ok: command.ok,
340	      validation: command.ok
341	        ? validValidation()
342	        : createConnectionValidationOutcome([
343	            createConnectionIssue(
344	              `connection.adapter.${command.error.kind}`,
345	              'adapter',
346	              command.error.message,
347	              'error',
348	            ),
349	          ]),
350	      snapshot,
351	      events,
352	      ...(error ? { error } : {}),
353	    };
354	  }
355	
356	  return {
357	    ...reader,
358	
359	    async connect(input) {
360	      const normalized = normalizeTransportConfig(input);
361	      if (!normalized.valid || !normalized.config) {
362	        return {
363	          ok: false,
364	          validation: normalized.issues.length > 0 ? normalized : invalidConfigValidation(),
365	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
366	          events: [],
367	        };
368	      }
369	
370	      state.upsertConfig(normalized.config);
371	      state.applyEvent({
372	        kind: 'connect-requested',
373	        connectionId: normalized.config.id,
374	        occurredAt: now(),
375	      });
376	
377	      const command = await options.adapter.connect(normalized.config);
378	      return applyAdapterOutcome(normalized.config.id, command);
379	    },
380	
381	    async disconnect(connectionId) {
382	      clearReconnectTimer(connectionId);
383	      resetReconnectState(connectionId);
384	
385	      state.applyEvent({
386	        kind: 'disconnect-requested',
387	        connectionId,
388	        occurredAt: now(),
389	      });
390	
391	      const command = await options.adapter.disconnect(connectionId);
392	      return applyAdapterOutcome(connectionId, command);
393	    },
394	
395	    async write(request) {
396	      state.applyEvent({
397	        kind: 'write-requested',
398	        connectionId: request.connectionId,
399	        occurredAt: now(),
400	        byteLength: request.bytes.length,
401	      });
402	
403	      const command = await options.adapter.write(request);
404	      return applyAdapterOutcome(request.connectionId, command);
405	    },
406	
407	    async drainAdapterEvents() {
408	      const beforeLength = state.getSnapshot().events.length;
409	      const events = await options.adapter.drainEvents();
410	      applyEvents(events);
411	
412	      // Check for disconnected events and initiate reconnect if applicable
413	      const disconnectedIds = events
414	        .filter((e) => e.kind === 'disconnected')
415	        .map((e) => e.connectionId);
416	
417	      for (const connectionId of disconnectedIds) {
418	        const fact = state.getSnapshot().runtimeFacts.find(
419	          (f) => f.connectionId === connectionId,
420	        );
421	        if (!fact) continue;
422	
423	        const policy = getReconnectPolicy(fact.kind);
424	        const currentAttempt = fact.reconnectAttempt ?? 0;
425	
426	        if (shouldReconnect(policy, currentAttempt)) {
427	          scheduleReconnect(
428	            connectionId,
429	            fact.config,
430	            currentAttempt,
431	            beforeLength,
432	          );
433	        } else if (policy.enabled) {
434	          // policy enabled but attempts exhausted
435	          state.applyEvent({
436	            kind: 'reconnect-exhausted',
437	            connectionId,
438	            occurredAt: now(),
439	          });
440	        }
441	        // if policy not enabled, nothing to do
442	      }
443	
444	      const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
445	      const allNewEvents = collectEventsAfter(beforeLength, snapshot);
446	
447	      return {
448	        ok: true,
449	        validation: validValidation(),
450	        snapshot,
451	        events: allNewEvents,
452	        ...(selectLastTransportError(snapshot)
453	          ? { error: selectLastTransportError(snapshot) }
454	          : {}),
455	      };
456	    },
457	
458	    async cleanup() {
459	      clearAllReconnectTimers();
460	
461	      const facts = state.getSnapshot().runtimeFacts;
462	      for (const fact of facts) {
463	        state.applyEvent({
464	          kind: 'cleanup',
465	          connectionId: fact.connectionId,
466	          occurredAt: now(),
467	        });
468	      }
469	
470	      // Reset reconnect state after cleanup events
471	      for (const fact of facts) {
472	        if (fact.reconnectNextAt !== undefined) {
473	          state.applyEvent({
474	            kind: 'reconnect-exhausted',
475	            connectionId: fact.connectionId,
476	            occurredAt: now(),
477	          });
478	        }
479	      }
480	
481	      const command = await options.adapter.cleanup();
482	      return applyAdapterOutcome('cleanup', command);
483	    },
484	  };
485	}
486

3	export const TRANSPORT_KINDS = ['serial', 'tcp-client', 'tcp-server', 'udp'] as const;

160	export type { ValidationIssue as ConnectionValidationIssue } from '@/shared/types/validation';
161	export type { ValidationResult as ConnectionValidationOutcome } from '@/shared/types/validation';
162	
163	export interface ConnectionConfigNormalization extends ConnectionValidationOutcome {
164	  readonly config?: TransportConfig;
165	}

167	export interface NormalizedTransportEventInput {

```
1	import type { TransportKind } from './types';
2	
3	export interface ReconnectPolicy {
4	  readonly enabled: boolean;
5	  readonly maxAttempts: number;
6	  readonly initialDelayMs: number;
7	  readonly maxDelayMs: number;
8	  readonly backoffMultiplier: number;
9	}
10	
11	const SERIAL_POLICY: ReconnectPolicy = {
12	  enabled: false,
13	  maxAttempts: 0,
14	  initialDelayMs: 0,
15	  maxDelayMs: 0,
16	  backoffMultiplier: 0,
17	};
18	
19	const TCP_CLIENT_POLICY: ReconnectPolicy = {
20	  enabled: true,
21	  maxAttempts: 10,
22	  initialDelayMs: 1000,
23	  maxDelayMs: 30000,
24	  backoffMultiplier: 2.0,
25	};
26	
27	const TCP_SERVER_POLICY: ReconnectPolicy = {
28	  enabled: true,
29	  maxAttempts: 10,
30	  initialDelayMs: 1000,
31	  maxDelayMs: 30000,
32	  backoffMultiplier: 2.0,
33	};
34	
35	const UDP_POLICY: ReconnectPolicy = {
36	  enabled: true,
37	  maxAttempts: 5,
38	  initialDelayMs: 1000,
39	  maxDelayMs: 10000,
40	  backoffMultiplier: 2.0,
41	};
42	
43	const POLICIES: Readonly<Record<TransportKind, ReconnectPolicy>> = {
44	  serial: SERIAL_POLICY,
45	  'tcp-client': TCP_CLIENT_POLICY,
46	  'tcp-server': TCP_SERVER_POLICY,
47	  udp: UDP_POLICY,
48	};
49	
50	export function getReconnectPolicy(kind: TransportKind): ReconnectPolicy {
51	  return POLICIES[kind];
52	}
53	
54	export function nextReconnectDelay(policy: ReconnectPolicy, attempt: number): number {
55	  const raw = policy.initialDelayMs * policy.backoffMultiplier ** attempt;
56	  return Math.min(raw, policy.maxDelayMs);
57	}
58	
59	export function shouldReconnect(policy: ReconnectPolicy, attempt: number): boolean {
60	  if (!policy.enabled) return false;
61	  if (policy.maxAttempts === 0) return true;
62	  return attempt < policy.maxAttempts;
63	}
64

  "old_string": "export function createIssue(\n  code: string,\n  path: string,\n  message: string,\n  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',\n): ValidationIssue {\n  return issue(code, path, message, severity);\n}",
  "new_string": "export function createIssue(\n  code: string,\n  path: string,\n  message: string,\n  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',\n): ValidationIssue {\n  return issue(code, path, message, severity);\n}\n\nexport function isLegacyFrameConfigJson(value: unknown): boolean {\n  return Array.isArray(value) && value.every((item) => isRecordCheck(item) && Array.isArray(item.fields));\n}\n\nexport function migrateLegacyFrameConfig(value: unknown): LegacyFrameMigrationResult {\n  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];\n\n  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {\n    return {\n      recognized: false,\n      frames: [],\n      issues: [createIssue('legacy.rootInvalid', 'legacy', '旧 frame 导入内容必须是数组')],\n    };\n  }\n\n  const frames = value\n    .map((item, index) => normalizeFrame(item, index, issues))\n    .filter((frame): frame is FrameAsset => frame !== undefined);\n\n  const validation = validateFrameAssetCollection(frames);\n\n  return {\n    recognized: true,\n    frames,\n    issues: [...issues, ...validation.issues],\n  };\n}"
}

117	export function createFrameAssetService(
118	  state: FrameStateContainer = createFrameState(),
119	): FrameAssetService {
120	  const reader = createFrameAssetReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
121	
122	  return {
123	    ...reader,
124	
125	    replaceFrames(frames, selectedFrameId) {
126	      const validation = validateFrameAssetCollection(frames);
127	      if (!validation.valid) {
128	        return {
129	          ok: false,
130	          validation,
131	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
132	        };
133	      }
134	
135	      return {
136	        ok: true,
137	        validation,
138	        snapshot: state.replaceFrames(frames, selectedFrameId),
139	      };
140	    },
141	
142	    loadLegacyFrameConfig(value, selectedFrameId) {
143	      const migration = migrateLegacyFrameConfig(value);
144	      if (!migration.recognized || hasError(migration.issues)) {
145	        return {
146	          ok: false,
147	          validation: {
148	            valid: !hasError(migration.issues),
149	            issues: migration.issues,
150	          },
151	          migration,
152	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
153	        };
154	      }
155	
156	      return {
157	        ok: true,
158	        validation: {
159	          valid: true,
160	          issues: migration.issues,
161	        },
162	        migration,
163	        snapshot: state.replaceFrames(migration.frames, selectedFrameId),
164	      };
165	    },
166	
167	    selectFrame(frameId) {
168	      if (frameId === undefined) {
169	        return {
170	          ok: true,
171	          validation: validResult(),
172	          snapshot: state.selectFrame(undefined),
173	        };
174	      }
175	
176	      if (!getFrameAsset(state.getSnapshot(), frameId)) {
177	        return {
178	          ok: false,
179	          validation: invalidResult([
180	            issue('frame.selectionMissing', 'selectedFrameId', `未找到帧: ${frameId}`),
181	          ]),
182	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
183	        };
184	      }
185	
186	      return {
187	        ok: true,
188	        validation: validResult(),
189	        snapshot: state.selectFrame(frameId),
190	      };
191	    },
192	  };
193	}
