逐函数分析 conditional spread：

1. **`cloneTransportConfig`**：
   - `label` — `BaseTransportConfig` 类型定义 `label?: string`（optional），所以 conditional spread 是正确的
   - serial/tcp-client/tcp-server 的 `label` 确实是 optional
   - udp 的 `remoteHost` 和 `remotePort` 也是 optional
   - 这些 conditional spread **都保护真正的 optional 字段**，不应简化

2. **`cloneTransportError`**：
   - `connectionId?: string` — optional
   - `recoverable?: boolean` — optional
   - 正确的 conditional spread

3. **`cloneTransportEvent`**：
   - `target?:` — optional
   - `byteLength?:` — optional
   - `error?:` — optional
   - `reconnectAttempt?:` — optional
   - `reconnectNextAt?:` — optional
   - 正确的 conditional spread

4. **`cloneConnectionRuntimeFact`**：
   - `lastActivityAt?:` — optional
   - `lastError?:` — optional
   - `reconnectAttempt?:` — optional
   - `reconnectNextAt?:` — optional
   - 正确的 conditional spread

5. **`cloneConnectionStateSnapshot`**：
   - `lastError?:` — optional
   - 正确的 conditional spread

**结论**：所有 conditional spread 都保护真正 optional 字段。但 spread `{...obj}` 对 optional 字段会保留 `undefined` 值（key 存在但值为 undefined），而 conditional spread `...(obj ? {key: obj} : {})` 会省略 key。对于 `readonly` 类型的深拷贝场景，省略 undefined key 更干净。

实际上，这些 clone 函数在做**选择性深拷贝** — 对嵌套对象递归 clone，对 primitive 字段直接赋值。conditional spread 确保输出不包含源对象中不存在的 key。这是正确行为。

**不需要简化 conditional spread**，它们都在保护真正 optional 的字段。但可以优化为更简洁的写法 — 使用 `structuredClone` 不行（语义差异），但可以在函数开头用一个通用的 pattern。

既然所有 conditional spread 都是必要的，C-S1 的正确结论是：**保留文件，不做改动**。但文件标题说"简化"，我们可以把代码做微小的可读性改善——不需要，因为当前代码已经很清晰。

handleReconnectTimerFired(\n    connectionId: string,\n    config: TransportConfig,\n    attempt: number,\n    policy: ReturnType<typeof getReconnectPolicy>,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      const command = await options.adapter.connect(config);\n      if (command.ok) {\n        for (const event of command.events ?? []) {\n          state.applyEvent(adapterEventToNormalized(event, now));\n        }\n        // connected event already resets reconnectAttempt/nextAt via reducer\n      } else {\n        // connect failed, increment attempt and try again\n        for (const event of command.events ?? []) {\n          state.applyEvent(adapterEventToNormalized(event, now));\n        }\n        if (command.events === undefined) {\n          state.applyEvent({\n            kind: 'error',\n            connectionId,\n            occurredAt: now(),\n            error: command.error,\n          });\n        }\n\n        const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;\n        if (shouldReconnect(policy, nextAttempt)) {\n          scheduleReconnect(connectionId, config, nextAttempt, state.getSnapshot().events.length);\n        } else {\n          state.applyEvent({\n            kind: 'reconnect-exhausted',\n            connectionId,\n            occurredAt: now(),\n          });\n        }\n      }\n    } catch {\n      const nextAttempt = attempt + 1;\n      if (shouldReconnect(policy, nextAttempt)) {\n        scheduleReconnect(connectionId, config, nextAttempt, state.getSnapshot().events.length);\n      } else {\n        state.applyEvent({\n          kind: 'reconnect-exhausted',\n          connectionId,\n          occurredAt: now(),\n        });\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  function applyAdapterOutcome(\n    connectionId: string,\n    command: ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome,\n  ): ConnectionOperationOutcome {\n    const beforeLength = state.getSnapshot().events.length;\n\n    applyEvents(command.events ?? []);\n    if (!command.ok && command.events === undefined) {\n      state.applyEvent(toErrorEvent(connectionId, command.error, now()));\n    }\n\n    const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();\n    const events = collectEventsAfter(beforeLength, snapshot);\n    const lastEvent = events[events.length - 1];\n    const error = !command.ok\n      ? adapterErrorToSnapshot(command.error, connectionId, lastEvent?.occurredAt ?? now())\n      : undefined;\n\n    return {\n      ok: command.ok,\n      validation: command.ok\n        ? validValidation()\n        : createConnectionValidationOutcome([\n            createConnectionIssue(\n              `connection.adapter.${command.error.kind}`,\n              'adapter',\n              command.error.message,\n              'error',\n            ),\n          ]),\n      snapshot,\n      events,\n      ...(error ? { error } : {}),\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    ...reader,\n\n    async connect(input) {\n      const normalized = normalizeTransportConfig(input);\n      if (!normalized.valid || !normalized.config) {\n        return {\n          ok: false,\n          validation: normalized.issues.length > 0 ? normalized : invalidConfigValidation(),\n          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),\n          events: [],\n        };\n      }\n\n      state.upsertConfig(normalized.config);\n      state.applyEvent({\n        kind: 'connect-requested',\n        connectionId: normalized.config.id,\n        occurredAt: now(),\n      });\n\n      const command = await options.adapter.connect(normalized.config);\n      return applyAdapterOutcome(normalized.config.id, command);\n    },\n\n    async disconnect(connectionId) {\n      clearReconnectTimer(connectionId);\n      resetReconnectState(connectionId);\n\n      state.applyEvent({\n        kind: 'disconnect-requested',\n        connectionId,\n        occurredAt: now(),\n      });\n\n      const command = await options.adapter.disconnect(connectionId);\n      return applyAdapterOutcome(connectionId, command);\n    },\n\n    async write(request) {\n      state.applyEvent({\n        kind: 'write-requested',\n        connectionId: request.connectionId,\n        occurredAt: now(),\n        byteLength: request.bytes.length,\n      });\n\n      const command = await options.adapter.write(request);\n      return applyAdapterOutcome(request.connectionId, command);\n    },\n\n    async drainAdapterEvents() {\n      const beforeLength = state.getSnapshot().events.length;\n      const events = await options.adapter.drainEvents();\n      applyEvents(events);\n\n      // Check for disconnected events and initiate reconnect if applicable\n      const disconnectedIds = events\n        .filter((e) => e.kind === 'disconnected')\n        .map((e) => e.connectionId);\n\n      for (const connectionId of disconnectedIds) {\n        const fact = state.getSnapshot().runtimeFacts.find(\n          (f) => f.connectionId === connectionId,\n        );\n        if (!fact) continue;\n\n        const policy = getReconnectPolicy(fact.kind);\n        const currentAttempt = fact.reconnectAttempt ?? 0;\n\n        if (shouldReconnect(policy, currentAttempt)) {\n          scheduleReconnect(\n            connectionId,\n            fact.config,\n            currentAttempt,\n            beforeLength,\n          );\n        } else if (policy.enabled) {\n          // policy enabled but attempts exhausted\n          state.applyEvent({\n            kind: 'reconnect-exhausted',\n            connectionId,\n            occurredAt: now(),\n          });\n        }\n        // if policy not enabled, nothing to do\n      }\n\n      const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();\n      const allNewEvents = collectEventsAfter(beforeLength, snapshot);\n      const lastError = snapshot.lastError ? cloneTransportError(snapshot.lastError) : undefined;\n\n      return {\n        ok: true,\n        validation: validValidation(),\n        snapshot,\n        events: allNewEvents,\n        ...(lastError ? { error: lastError } : {}),\n      };\n    },\n\n    async cleanup() {\n      clearAllReconnectTimers();\n\n      const facts = state.getSnapshot().runtimeFacts;\n      for (const fact of facts) {\n        state.applyEvent({\n          kind: 'cleanup',\n          connectionId: fact.connectionId,\n          occurredAt: now(),\n        });\n      }\n\n      // Reset reconnect state after cleanup events\n      for (const fact of facts) {\n        if (fact.reconnectNextAt !== undefined) {\n          state.applyEvent({\n            kind: 'reconnect-exhausted',\n            connectionId: fact.connectionId,\n            occurredAt: now(),\n          });\n        }\n      }\n\n      const command = await options.adapter.cleanup();\n      return applyAdapterOutcome('cleanup', command);\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
```json
}
```

> AGENT

`normalizeTransportConfig` 接受 `unknown`，是对原始输入做 normalize 的逻辑。如果入参收紧为 `TransportConfig`，则已经通过了 TypeScript 类型检查，但运行时仍需 validate（比如 enum 约束）。

C-R1 的要求是签名收紧。但 service 现有逻辑先 normalize 再 connect，normalize 是安全网。收紧签名后：
- 类型签名：`connect(config: TransportConfig)`
- 内部仍调用 `normalizeTransportConfig`（传入 `unknown` 是兼容的，TransportConfig 也可以作为 unknown 传入）
- 或者可以跳过 normalize 直接使用 config

看任务描述："connect(config: unknown) → connect(config: TransportConfig)，内部 normalizeTransportConfig 不变"。所以只改签名。

> TOOL

> AGENT

当前 connect 流程：
1. normalizeTransportConfig → 得到 config
2. state.upsertConfig(config) — 先写 config
3. state.applyEvent('connect-requested') — 再写事件
4. adapter.connect(config) — 调 adapter
5. applyAdapterOutcome — 写结果

问题：步骤 2-3 先写 state，如果步骤 4 失败，config 和 connect-requested 事件会留在 state 中（幽灵 config）。

改为：
1. normalizeTransportConfig → 得到 config
2. adapter.connect(config) — 先调 adapter
3. 成功后 → state.upsertConfig + applyEvent('connect-requested') + applyAdapterOutcome
4. 失败 → 不写任何 state，直接返回失败结果

但注意：applyAdapterOutcome 内部也写 state（写 adapter 返回的 events）。如果 adapter connect 失败，adapter 可能返回 error events。原子化意味着失败时不写任何 state。

看 applyAdapterOutcome 的逻辑——它总是写 events 到 state。所以原子化需要在 connect 方法中不调用 applyAdapterOutcome，而是自己处理。

> TOOL

这个测试检查：
- `outcome.events.map((event) => event.kind).toEqual(['error'])` — 原来失败时 state 写了 error event，所以 collectEventsAfter 能拿到。现在失败时不写 state，events 为空数组。
- `service.getConnectionFact(...)` — 原来失败时写了 state，有 runtimeFact。现在不写 state，应该返回 undefined。

需要更新测试以匹配新的原子化行为。

```
550	    async cleanup() {
551	      clearAllReconnectTimers();
552	
553	      const facts = state.getSnapshot().runtimeFacts;
554	      for (const fact of facts) {
555	        state.applyEvent({
556	          kind: 'cleanup',
557	          connectionId: fact.connectionId,
558	          occurredAt: now(),
559	        });
560	      }
561	
562	      // Reset reconnect state after cleanup events

567	            connectionId: fact.connectionId,
```

```json
  "old_string": "export function cloneTransportConfig(config: ReadonlyTransportConfig): TransportConfig {\n  switch (config.kind) {\n    case 'serial':\n      return {\n        id: config.id,\n        kind: config.kind,\n        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),\n        portPath: config.portPath,\n        baudRate: config.baudRate,\n      };\n    case 'tcp-client':\n      return {\n        id: config.id,\n        kind: config.kind,\n        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),\n        host: config.host,\n        port: config.port,\n      };\n    case 'tcp-server':\n      return {\n        id: config.id,\n        kind: config.kind,\n        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),\n        host: config.host,\n        port: config.port,\n      };\n    case 'udp':\n      return {\n        id: config.id,\n        kind: config.kind,\n        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),\n        localHost: config.localHost,\n        localPort: config.localPort,\n        ...(config.remoteHost ? { remoteHost: config.remoteHost } : {}),\n        ...(config.remotePort !== undefined ? { remotePort: config.remotePort } : {}),\n      };\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "export function cloneTransportConfig(config: ReadonlyTransportConfig): TransportConfig {\n  switch (config.kind) {\n    case 'serial':\n      return {\n        id: config.id,\n        kind: config.kind,\n        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),\n        ...(config.autoConnect !== undefined ? { autoConnect: config.autoConnect } : {}),\n        portPath: config.portPath,\n        baudRate: config.baudRate,\n      };\n    case 'tcp-client':\n      return {\n        id: config.id,\n        kind: config.kind,\n        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),\n        ...(config.autoConnect !== undefined ? { autoConnect: config.autoConnect } : {}),\n        host: config.host,\n        port: config.port,\n      };\n    case 'tcp-server':\n      return {\n        id: config.id,\n        kind: config.kind,\n        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),\n        ...(config.autoConnect !== undefined ? { autoConnect: config.autoConnect } : {}),\n        host: config.host,\n        port: config.port,\n      };\n    case 'udp':\n      return {\n        id: config.id,\n        kind: config.kind,\n        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),\n        ...(config.autoConnect !== undefined ? { autoConnect: config.autoConnect } : {}),\n        localHost: config.localHost,\n        localPort: config.localPort,\n        ...(config.remoteHost ? { remoteHost: config.remoteHost } : {}),\n        ...(config.remotePort !== undefined ? { remotePort: config.remotePort } : {}),\n      };\n  }\n}"
```

## 5. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/frame/core/legacy.ts` (full)

```typescript
import { normalizeFrame, isRecordCheck, createIssue } from './legacy-normalizers';
import { validateFrameAssetCollection } from './validation-frame';
import type { FrameAsset, LegacyFrameMigrationResult, ValidationIssue } from './types';

export function isLegacyFrameConfigJson(value: unknown): boolean {
  return Array.isArray(value) && value.every((item) => isRecordCheck(item) && Array.isArray(item.fields));
}

export function migrateLegacyFrameConfig(value: unknown): LegacyFrameMigrationResult {
  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];

  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
    return {
      recognized: false,
      frames: [],
      issues: [createIssue('legacy.rootInvalid', 'legacy', '旧 frame 导入内容必须是数组')],
    };
  }

  const frames = value
    .map((item, index) => normalizeFrame(item, index, issues))
    .filter((frame): frame is FrameAsset => frame !== undefined);

  const validation = validateFrameAssetCollection(frames);

  return {
    recognized: true,
    frames,
    issues: [...issues, ...validation.issues],
  };
}
```

```typescript
} from './types';

type UnknownRecord = Record<string, unknown>;

function issue(
  code: string,
  path: string,
  message: string,
  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',
): ValidationIssue {
  return { code, path, message, severity };
}

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is UnknownRecord {
  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function stringValue(value: unknown, fallback = ''): string {
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : fallback;
}
```

```typescript
import {
  cloneConnectionRuntimeFact,
  cloneConnectionStateSnapshot,
  cloneTransportConfig,
  cloneTransportError,
  cloneTransportEvent,
  cloneTransportTarget,
  type ConnectionRuntimeFact,
  type ConnectionStateSnapshot,
  type ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
  type TransportConfig,
  type TransportErrorSnapshot,
  type TransportEventSnapshot,
  type TransportKind,
  type TransportTargetSnapshot,
} from '../core';

export interface ConnectionTargetQuery {
  readonly kind?: TransportKind;
  readonly availableOnly?: boolean;
}

export interface ConnectionSummary {
  readonly connectionId: string;
  readonly kind: TransportKind;
  readonly lifecycle: ConnectionRuntimeFact['lifecycle'];
  readonly label: string;
  readonly routeLabel: string;
  readonly available: boolean;
  readonly rxBytes: number;
  readonly txBytes: number;
  readonly errorCount: number;
  readonly lastActivityAt?: string;
  readonly lastError?: TransportErrorSnapshot;
}

function matchesTarget(
  target: TransportTargetSnapshot,
  query: ConnectionTargetQuery | undefined,
): boolean {
  if (query?.kind && target.kind !== query.kind) {
    return false;
  }

  return query?.availableOnly ? target.available : true;
}

function toSummary(fact: ConnectionRuntimeFact): ConnectionSummary {
  return {
    connectionId: fact.connectionId,
    kind: fact.kind,
    lifecycle: fact.lifecycle,
    label: fact.target.label,
    routeLabel: fact.target.routeLabel,
    available: fact.target.available,
    rxBytes: fact.counters.rxBytes,
    txBytes: fact.counters.txBytes,
    errorCount: fact.counters.errorCount,
    ...(fact.lastActivityAt ? { lastActivityAt: fact.lastActivityAt } : {}),
    ...(fact.lastError ? { lastError: cloneTransportError(fact.lastError) } : {}),
  };
}

export function selectConnectionSnapshot(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): ConnectionStateSnapshot {
  return cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(snapshot);
}

export function selectTransportConfigs(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): TransportConfig[] {
  return snapshot.configs.map(cloneTransportConfig);
}

export function selectConnectionFacts(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): ConnectionRuntimeFact[] {
  return snapshot.runtimeFacts.map(cloneConnectionRuntimeFact);
}

export function selectConnectionFact(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
  connectionId: string,
): ConnectionRuntimeFact | undefined {
  const fact = snapshot.runtimeFacts.find((item) => item.connectionId === connectionId);
  return fact ? cloneConnectionRuntimeFact(fact) : undefined;
}

export function selectConnectionSummaries(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): ConnectionSummary[] {
  return snapshot.runtimeFacts.map(toSummary);
}

export function selectTransportTargets(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
  query?: ConnectionTargetQuery,
): TransportTargetSnapshot[] {
  return snapshot.runtimeFacts
    .map((fact) => fact.target)
    .filter((target) => matchesTarget(target, query))
    .map(cloneTransportTarget);
}

export function selectLastTransportError(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): TransportErrorSnapshot | undefined {
  return snapshot.lastError ? cloneTransportError(snapshot.lastError) : undefined;
}

export function selectTransportEvents(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): TransportEventSnapshot[] {
  return snapshot.events.map(cloneTransportEvent);
}
```

## 13. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/core/clone.ts` (full)

```typescript
import type {
  ConnectionCounterSnapshot,
  ConnectionRuntimeFact,
  ConnectionStateSnapshot,
  ReadonlyConnectionRuntimeFact,
  ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
  ReadonlyTransportConfig,
  ReadonlyTransportEventSnapshot,
  ReadonlyTransportTargetSnapshot,
  TransportConfig,
  TransportErrorSnapshot,
  TransportEventSnapshot,
  TransportTargetSnapshot,
} from './types';

export function cloneTransportConfig(config: ReadonlyTransportConfig): TransportConfig {
  switch (config.kind) {
    case 'serial':
      return {
        id: config.id,
        kind: config.kind,
        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),
        portPath: config.portPath,
        baudRate: config.baudRate,
      };
    case 'tcp-client':
      return {
        id: config.id,
        kind: config.kind,
        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),
        host: config.host,
        port: config.port,
      };
    case 'tcp-server':
      return {
        id: config.id,
        kind: config.kind,
        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),
        host: config.host,
        port: config.port,
      };
    case 'udp':
      return {
        id: config.id,
        kind: config.kind,
        ...(config.label ? { label: config.label } : {}),
        localHost: config.localHost,
        localPort: config.localPort,
        ...(config.remoteHost ? { remoteHost: config.remoteHost } : {}),
        ...(config.remotePort !== undefined ? { remotePort: config.remotePort } : {}),
      };
  }
}

export function cloneTransportConfigs(
  configs: readonly ReadonlyTransportConfig[],
): TransportConfig[] {
  return configs.map(cloneTransportConfig);
}

export function cloneConnectionCounters(
  counters: Readonly<ConnectionCounterSnapshot>,
): ConnectionCounterSnapshot {
  return { ...counters };
}

export function cloneTransportTarget(
  target: ReadonlyTransportTargetSnapshot,
): TransportTargetSnapshot {
  return { ...target };
}

export function cloneTransportError(error: Readonly<TransportErrorSnapshot>): TransportErrorSnapshot {
  return {
    kind: error.kind,
    message: error.message,
    occurredAt: error.occurredAt,
    ...(error.connectionId ? { connectionId: error.connectionId } : {}),
    ...(error.recoverable !== undefined ? { recoverable: error.recoverable } : {}),
  };
}

export function cloneTransportEvent(
  event: ReadonlyTransportEventSnapshot,
): TransportEventSnapshot {
  return {
    id: event.id,
    kind: event.kind,
    connectionId: event.connectionId,
    occurredAt: event.occurredAt,
    ...(event.target ? { target: cloneTransportTarget(event.target) } : {}),
    ...(event.byteLength !== undefined ? { byteLength: event.byteLength } : {}),
    ...(event.error ? { error: cloneTransportError(event.error) } : {}),
  };
}

export function cloneConnectionRuntimeFact(
  fact: ReadonlyConnectionRuntimeFact,
): ConnectionRuntimeFact {
  return {
    connectionId: fact.connectionId,
    kind: fact.kind,
    lifecycle: fact.lifecycle,
    config: cloneTransportConfig(fact.config),
    target: cloneTransportTarget(fact.target),
    counters: cloneConnectionCounters(fact.counters),
    ...(fact.lastActivityAt ? { lastActivityAt: fact.lastActivityAt } : {}),
    ...(fact.lastError ? { lastError: cloneTransportError(fact.lastError) } : {}),
  };
}

export function cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): ConnectionStateSnapshot {
  return {
    schemaVersion: snapshot.schemaVersion,
    configs: cloneTransportConfigs(snapshot.configs),
    runtimeFacts: snapshot.runtimeFacts.map(cloneConnectionRuntimeFact),
    events: snapshot.events.map(cloneTransportEvent),
    ...(snapshot.lastError ? { lastError: cloneTransportError(snapshot.lastError) } : {}),
  };
}
```

```
All changes are staged. Here is a summary of what was done:

---

**Changed files:**

- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/core/types.ts` -- Added `autoConnect?: boolean` to `BaseTransportConfig`. Added `ConnectionTargetQuery` and `ConnectionSummary` interfaces before `ReadonlyTransportTargetSnapshot`.

- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/core/index.ts` -- Added `ConnectionSummary` and `ConnectionTargetQuery` to the type exports.

- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/core/clone.ts` -- Added `autoConnect` conditional spread to all four transport config clone branches (serial, tcp-client, tcp-server, udp).

- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/services/connection-service.ts` -- Full rewrite: removed selector imports, inlined selector logic (matchesTarget, toSummary) as local functions, inlined reader methods calling clone functions directly, changed `connect(config: unknown)` to `connect(config: TransportConfig)`, atomized connect (adapter first, state write only on success), added `discoverResources()` method, added `ReconnectStatus` interface and `getReconnectStatus()` to `ConnectionReader`, replaced `selectLastTransportError` call in `drainAdapterEvents` with inline clone.

- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/services/index.ts` -- Added `ReconnectStatus` to re-exports.

- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/index.ts` -- Removed all selector exports, added `ConnectionSummary`/`ConnectionTargetQuery` from `./core`, added `ReconnectStatus` from `./services`, added `ConnectionResourceCandidate` to adapter type exports.

- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/adapters/ports.ts` -- Added optional `discoverResources?()` method to `ConnectionTransportAdapter` interface.

- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/__tests__/connection-state-service-selector.spec.ts` -- Replaced selector function imports with `ConnectionSummary` type from `../core`, updated tests to use service reader methods (`listConnectionSummaries`, `listTransportTargets`) instead of selector functions, updated atomized connect assertions (empty events, outcome.error, no state on adapter failure), cast `invalidTransportConfigSample` for typed `connect`, removed `selectTransportTargets` API property check.

- `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/__tests__/routing-tick.spec.ts` -- Added `getReconnectStatus` and `discoverResources` stubs to the `ConnectionService` mock.

- Deleted: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/selectors/` directory (connection-selectors.ts and index.ts).

**Verify evidence:**

- `pnpm -C rewrite lint`: 5 pre-existing errors in unrelated files (command-ingress, task, runtime), none in connection.
- TypeScript `tsc --noEmit`: connection-specific errors are all pre-existing (ReadonlyDeep resolution, reconnect-service tests referencing not-yet-committed fields, fake-adapter type narrowing). No new errors introduced.
- `vitest run` connection tests: 12 pass, 1 fail. The single failure (`createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter` in public API) is pre-existing -- the original `index.ts` already exported it before this change.

**Open issues:** none (all specified changes completed; pre-existing failures documented above).
```

> AGENT

问题在 line 16-22：测试直接传 `{ kind: 'serial', id: 'serial-1', portPath: '/dev/ttyUSB0', baudRate: 115200 }` 给 `connect()`。现在 `connect` 签名收紧为 `TransportConfig`，但更重要的是**原子化**：adapter 先 connect，成功后才写 state。

fake adapter 的 connect 返回 `{ ok: true, events: [{ kind: 'connected', ... }] }`。原子化后，成功时先 `upsertConfig` + `connect-requested` event，然后 apply adapter events（`connected` event）。

测试断言 `connectResult.ok === true`。如果 fake adapter 返回 ok，应该没问题。但问题是 `connect-requested` 和 `connected` 事件的顺序可能影响了 tick 结果。

让我看 routing tick 做了什么。

```
20	const emptySnapshot: ConnectionStateSnapshot = {

27	function okOutcome(
28	  events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[] = [],
29	): ConnectionOperationOutcome {
30	  return {
31	    ok: true,
32	    validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },
33	    snapshot: emptySnapshot,
34	    events,
35	  };
36	}
37	
38	function failOutcome(
39	  error: { kind: string; message: string },
40	): ConnectionOperationOutcome {
41	  return {
42	    ok: false,
43	    validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },
44	    snapshot: emptySnapshot,
45	    events: [],
46	    error: { kind: error.kind as any, message: error.message, occurredAt: '', connectionId: '', recoverable: false },
47	  };
48	}
49	
50	function dataEvent(
51	  connectionId: string,
52	  bytes: readonly number[],
53	): TransportEventSnapshot {
54	  return {
55	    id: `${connectionId}:data:2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z`,
56	    kind: 'data',
57	    connectionId,
58	    occurredAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
59	    bytes,
60	    byteLength: bytes.length,
61	  };
62	}
63	
64	function dataEventWithTarget(
65	  connectionId: string,
66	  bytes: readonly number[],
67	  target: TransportTargetSnapshot,
68	): TransportEventSnapshot {
69	  return {
70	    ...dataEvent(connectionId, bytes),
71	    target,
72	  };
73	}
```

> AGENT

feature-wiring 测试的 `stubConnectionService` 缺少 `getReconnectStatus` 和 `discoverResources`，但它用 `as ConnectionService` 类型断言绕过了。不过 line 102 `...overrides` 应该能覆盖。

问题是 `stubConnectionService` 的类型断言 `as ConnectionService` 是否在新接口下仍有效。TypeScript 的 `as` 是强制类型断言，编译不会报错。但运行时 `ConnectionService` 接口多了 `getReconnectStatus` 和 `discoverResources`，mock 没有这些方法。

让我看 bootstrap-integration 失败的具体原因——是运行时调用 `getReconnectStatus` 或 `discoverResources` 但 stub 没有。

```
1	import type { ConditionMatchInput } from '@/features/task';
2	import { ConnectionToReceiveInputSource } from './bridges/connection-to-receive';
3	import type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';
4	
5	export interface RoutingTickResult {
6	  readonly ok: boolean;
7	  readonly error?: string;
8	  readonly eventsRouted: number;
9	  readonly matchesEmitted: number;
10	}
11	
12	export async function routingTick(
13	  features: RewriteWiredFeatures,
14	): Promise<RoutingTickResult> {
15	  const drainOutcome =
16	    await features.connectionService.drainAdapterEvents();
17	
18	  if (!drainOutcome.ok) {
19	    return {
20	      ok: false,
21	      error: drainOutcome.error?.message ?? 'Connection drain failed',
22	      eventsRouted: 0,
23	      matchesEmitted: 0,
24	    };
25	  }
26	
27	  const dataEvents = drainOutcome.events.filter(
28	    (event) => event.kind === 'data' && event.bytes !== undefined,
29	  );
30	
31	  if (dataEvents.length === 0) {
32	    return { ok: true, eventsRouted: 0, matchesEmitted: 0 };
33	  }
34	
35	  const source = new ConnectionToReceiveInputSource(dataEvents);
36	  const receiveOutcome =
37	    await features.receiveService.drainInputSource(source);
38	
39	  if (!receiveOutcome.ok) {
40	    return {
41	      ok: false,
42	      error: 'Receive processing failed',
43	      eventsRouted: dataEvents.length,
44	      matchesEmitted: 0,
45	    };
46	  }
47	
48	  const matchInputs: ConditionMatchInput[] = [];
49	  for (const outcome of receiveOutcome.outcomes) {
50	    if (outcome.kind !== 'matched') continue;
51	    if (!outcome.matchedFrame) continue;
52	
53	    for (const field of outcome.fields) {
54	      const sourceId = outcome.input?.source.sourceId;
55	      matchInputs.push({
56	        frameId: outcome.matchedFrame.frameId,
57	        fieldId: field.fieldId,
58	        value: field.value as number | string | null,
59	        ...(sourceId ? { sourceId } : {}),
60	      });
61	    }
62	  }
63	
64	  if (matchInputs.length > 0) {
65	    features.receiveEventSourceBridge.emit(matchInputs);
66	  }
67	
68	  return {
69	    ok: true,
70	    eventsRouted: dataEvents.length,
71	    matchesEmitted: matchInputs.length,
72	  };
73	}
74	
```

> AGENT

drainAdapterEvents 返回 `ok: true`。那问题不在 drain。

等下，让我仔细看：bootstrap 测试先 connect（line 16-22），然后 pushData（line 25），然后 routingTick（line 26）。

connect 原子化后：adapter connect 成功 → upsertConfig + connect-requested → applyEvents(connected event)。这应该是对的。

但 bootstrap 测试的 connect 传的是 raw object：
```js
{ kind: 'serial', id: 'serial-1', portPath: '/dev/ttyUSB0', baudRate: 115200 }
```

签名收紧为 `TransportConfig` 后，TypeScript 类型检查应该通过（这符合 SerialTransportConfig）。

等一下——签名从 `unknown` 改为 `TransportConfig`，但测试传的 raw object 字面量在 TypeScript 中会被推断为 `{ kind: string; id: string; ... }` 而不是 `SerialTransportConfig`（因为 `kind: 'serial'` 是 string literal）。但测试文件可能没有严格类型检查。

Fake adapter 的 connect 返回 `{ ok: true, events: [{ kind: 'connected', ... }] }`。

原子化 connect 流程：
1. adapter.connect → `ok: true` + connected event
2. state.upsertConfig(config)
3. state.applyEvent({ kind: 'connect-requested', ... })
4. applyEvents(connected event)

但 `applyEvents` 里用的是 `state.applyEvent(adapterEventToNormalized(event, now))`。connected event 的 `connectionId` 是 `configCopy.id = 'serial-1'`。

`applyEvent` 调用 `reduceTransportEvent` 更新 runtimeFacts。但 reduceTransportEvent 需要 runtimeFact 存在（通过之前的 connect-requested 创建）。

等下！**顺序问题**：我先 `upsertConfig` + `connect-requested`（创建 runtimeFact），然后 `applyEvents(connected)`（更新 runtimeFact 到 connected）。这顺序是对的。

问题可能在于：原子化 connect 后，connect-requested 事件先被 apply 到 state，然后 connected 事件也被 apply。这些事件的 `beforeLength` 计数可能有问题。


connect 之后 state 中已有 connect-requested 和 connected 事件。然后 pushData 添加 data event 到 fake adapter queue。然后 drainAdapterEvents：
- beforeLength = 2 (connect-requested + connected)
- events = drainEvents from adapter → [data event]
- applyEvents → state 中加 data event
- collectEventsAfter(2, snapshot) → 只返回 data event

但 receive 代码没有改动。唯一变的是 connect 的原子化。让我看 `drainAdapterEvents` 中 events 是否包含了 data event。

原子化后 connect 成功时先写 connect-requested 和 connected events 到 state。然后 `collectEventsAfter(beforeLength)` 收集新增 events。drainAdapterEvents 的 beforeLength 是 2（connect-requested + connected 已在 state 中）。drainEvents 从 adapter 返回 [data event]。applyEvents 加到 state。collectEventsAfter(2) → 返回 [data event]。

但等下——在 HEAD 版本（非原子化），connect 流程是：
1. upsertConfig
2. applyEvent(connect-requested)
3. adapter.connect → { ok: true, events: [connected] }
4. applyAdapterOutcome → applyEvents([connected])

所以 HEAD 上 connect 完成后，state 中有 [connect-requested, connected] 事件。

然后 pushData 添加 data event 到 queue。drainAdapterEvents：
- beforeLength = 2
- drainEvents → [data event]  
- applyEvents → state 中加 data event
- collectEventsAfter(2) → [data event]

返回 ok=true, events=[data event]。

在我的原子化版本，connect 成功时：
1. adapter.connect → { ok: true, events: [connected] }
2. state.upsertConfig(config)
3. state.applyEvent(connect-requested) → state 有 [connect-requested]
4. beforeLength = 1
5. applyEvents([connected]) → state 有 [connect-requested, connected]
6. collectEventsAfter(1) → [connected]
7. return { ok: true, events: [connected] }

然后 pushData。drainAdapterEvents：
- beforeLength = 2
- drainEvents → [data event]
- applyEvents → state 有 [connect-requested, connected, data]
- collectEventsAfter(2) → [data event]
- 返回 ok=true, events=[data event]

行为应该和 HEAD 一样。除非 applyEvents 中的 `adapterEventToNormalized` 处理 connected event 时出了问题。

> AGENT

**3 个全绿！** 把 `kind: 'serial'` 加了 `as const` 后测试通过了。问题是 TypeScript 的 literal type 推断——不加 `as const` 时 `kind` 类型推断为 `string` 而不是 `'serial'`，导致 `normalizeTransportConfig` 可能无法正确识别。

但等下，原来 HEAD 上没加 `as const` 也能通过。区别在于签名从 `unknown` 变为 `TransportConfig`。

当签名是 `unknown` 时，raw object 字面量传入 `unknown` 参数完全合法。`normalizeTransportConfig` 内部对 `unknown` 做类型检查，包括 `kind` 检查。

当签名是 `TransportConfig` 时，TypeScript 要求传入值满足 `TransportConfig` 类型。`{ kind: 'serial', ... }` 中 `kind` 推断为 `string`，不是 `'serial'` 字面量类型，不满足 `TransportKind`（`'serial' | 'tcp-client' | ...`）。但因为这是测试文件且 vitest 可能不做严格类型检查...

实际上 vitest 通过 `tsx` 或 `esbuild` 转译，不做类型检查。所以 TypeScript 类型不影响运行时。运行时 `kind` 的值仍然是 `'serial'`，`normalizeTransportConfig` 应该能处理。

但测试加了 debug 行后就通过了？让我移除 debug 行再试。

> AGENT

仍然失败。加 `as const` 不够。但加了中间的 `drainAdapterEvents` 调用后就通过了。

关键区别：在 `tickResult` 之前调了一次 `drainAdapterEvents`。这意味着 fake adapter queue 中有 data event，但 **如果没有显式调 drain，data event 会留在 queue 中**，然后在 routingTick 的 drain 中被取出。

但等下——routingTick 自己也调 `drainAdapterEvents`。第一次显式调 drain 取走了 data event，routingTick 再调 drain 时 queue 已空，所以没有 data events。routingTick 返回 ok=true（因为没有 data events 不算失败）。

所以**真正的问题是 routingTick 中 data event 被取到了但没有被正确传递**。或者更可能的是：routingTick 中的 drain 成功了，但 receive 端有问题。

让我在 routingTick 里加临时 debug。

```json
  "content": "import type { ConditionMatchInput } from '@/features/task';\nimport { ConnectionToReceiveInputSource } from './bridges/connection-to-receive';\nimport type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';\n\nexport interface RoutingTickResult {\n  readonly ok: boolean;\n  readonly error?: string;\n  readonly eventsRouted: number;\n  readonly matchesEmitted: number;\n}\n\nexport async function routingTick(\n  features: RewriteWiredFeatures,\n): Promise<RoutingTickResult> {\n  const drainOutcome =\n    await features.connectionService.drainAdapterEvents();\n\n  if (!drainOutcome.ok) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      error: drainOutcome.error?.message ?? 'Connection drain failed',\n      eventsRouted: 0,\n      matchesEmitted: 0,\n    };\n  }\n\n  const dataEvents = drainOutcome.events.filter(\n    (event) => event.kind === 'data' && event.bytes !== undefined,\n  );\n\n  if (dataEvents.length === 0) {\n    return { ok: true, eventsRouted: 0, matchesEmitted: 0 };\n  }\n\n  const source = new ConnectionToReceiveInputSource(dataEvents);\n  const receiveOutcome =\n    await features.receiveService.drainInputSource(source);\n\n  if (!receiveOutcome.ok) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      error: `Receive processing failed: ${receiveOutcome.issues.map(i => i.message).join(', ')}`,\n      eventsRouted: dataEvents.length,\n      matchesEmitted: 0,\n    };\n  }\n\n  const matchInputs: ConditionMatchInput[] = [];\n  for (const outcome of receiveOutcome.outcomes) {\n    if (outcome.kind !== 'matched') continue;\n    if (!outcome.matchedFrame) continue;\n\n    for (const field of outcome.fields) {\n      const sourceId = outcome.input?.source.sourceId;\n      matchInputs.push({\n        frameId: outcome.matchedFrame.frameId,\n        fieldId: field.fieldId,\n        value: field.value as number | string | null,\n        ...(sourceId ? { sourceId } : {}),\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (matchInputs.length > 0) {\n    features.receiveEventSourceBridge.emit(matchInputs);\n  }\n\n  return {\n    ok: true,\n    eventsRouted: dataEvents.length,\n    matchesEmitted: matchInputs.length,\n  };\n}\n"
```

`processReceiveBatch` 处理输入。如果没有帧定义，应该返回 unmatched results。不应该失败。

等下——`drainInputSource` 调用 `source.drainEvents()`。`ConnectionToReceiveInputSource.drainEvents()` 过滤 `event.bytes !== undefined`，返回 `{ kind: 'batch', batch: ... }` 事件。

但 `batch` 需要 `id`、`bytes`、`receivedAt`、`source`。如果 data event 的 `id` 是 undefined...

> AGENT

frame-matcher.ts 是 tracked 的，但在 HEAD 中。所以 "No receive frame reference" 错误在 HEAD 上也存在。但 HEAD 上 bootstrap 测试通过了。

区别一定在 connect 行为上。HEAD 上 connect 先写 state（upsertConfig + connect-requested），然后 adapter.connect。如果 adapter.connect 成功，connected event 被写入 state。drainAdapterEvents 后取到 data event。

在我的版本，connect 先 adapter.connect，成功后写 state。区别在于 **connect 返回的 events** 不同：
- HEAD: connect 返回 [connect-requested, connected]
- 我的: connect 返回 [connected]

但 bootstrap 测试不关心 connect 返回的 events——只关心 ok。

问题可能更微妙。让我恢复 bootstrap 测试到原样（不加 debug），然后**回退 connect 到非原子化版本**看是否通过。

> AGENT

当 `matchReceiveFrame` 没有匹配到帧时，返回 `{ issues: [...] }` 无 `matchedFrame`。然后检查 `matchIssuesAreConfigErrors`。如果是 config error，`kind = 'config-error'`；否则 `kind = 'unmatched'`。

`outcomeIsOk` 检查 `kind === 'matched' || kind === 'unmatched'`。`config-error` 不匹配！所以 `ok` 变成 `false`。

`'receive.frame.none'` code 匹配 "No receive frame reference" 错误。所以 `matchIssuesAreConfigErrors` 返回 true → `kind = 'config-error'` → `outcomeIsOk` 返回 false → `serviceOutcome.ok = false`。

```
207	
208	    applyEvents(command.events ?? []);

212

214	    const events = collectEventsAfter(beforeLength, snapshot);

217	      ? adapterErrorToSnapshot(command.error, connectionId, lastEvent?.occurredAt ?? now())
218	      : undefined;
219	
220	    return {

222	      validation: command.ok

227	              'adapter',
228	              command.error.message,

233	      events,
234	      ...(error ? { error } : {}),

252	      state.upsertConfig(normalized.config);

310	      }
311	
312	      const command = await options.adapter.cleanup();
313	      return applyAdapterOutcome('cleanup', command);
314	    },
315	  };
316	}
317	
```

```
import {
  cloneConnectionRuntimeFact,
  cloneConnectionStateSnapshot,
  cloneTransportConfig,
  cloneTransportError,
  cloneTransportEvent,
  cloneTransportTarget,
  type ConnectionRuntimeFact,
  type ConnectionStateSnapshot,
  type ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
  type TransportConfig,
  type TransportErrorSnapshot,
  type TransportEventSnapshot,
  type TransportKind,
  type TransportTargetSnapshot,
} from '../core';

export interface ConnectionTargetQuery {
  readonly kind?: TransportKind;
  readonly availableOnly?: boolean;
}

export interface ConnectionSummary {
  readonly connectionId: string;
  readonly kind: TransportKind;
  readonly lifecycle: ConnectionRuntimeFact['lifecycle'];
  readonly label: string;
  readonly routeLabel: string;
  readonly available: boolean;
  readonly rxBytes: number;
  readonly txBytes: number;
  readonly errorCount: number;
  readonly lastActivityAt?: string;
  readonly lastError?: TransportErrorSnapshot;
}

function matchesTarget(
  target: TransportTargetSnapshot,
  query: ConnectionTargetQuery | undefined,
): boolean {
  if (query?.kind && target.kind !== query.kind) {
    return false;
  }

  return query?.availableOnly ? target.available : true;
}

function toSummary(fact: ConnectionRuntimeFact): ConnectionSummary {
  return {
    connectionId: fact.connectionId,
    kind: fact.kind,
    lifecycle: fact.lifecycle,
    label: fact.target.label,
    routeLabel: fact.target.routeLabel,
    available: fact.target.available,
    rxBytes: fact.counters.rxBytes,
    txBytes: fact.counters.txBytes,
    errorCount: fact.counters.errorCount,
    ...(fact.lastActivityAt ? { lastActivityAt: fact.lastActivityAt } : {}),
    ...(fact.lastError ? { lastError: cloneTransportError(fact.lastError) } : {}),
  };
}

export function selectConnectionSnapshot(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): ConnectionStateSnapshot {
  return cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(snapshot);
}

export function selectTransportConfigs(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): TransportConfig[] {
  return snapshot.configs.map(cloneTransportConfig);
}

export function selectConnectionFacts(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): ConnectionRuntimeFact[] {
  return snapshot.runtimeFacts.map(cloneConnectionRuntimeFact);
}

export function selectConnectionFact(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
  connectionId: string,
): ConnectionRuntimeFact | undefined {
  const fact = snapshot.runtimeFacts.find((item) => item.connectionId === connectionId);
  return fact ? cloneConnectionRuntimeFact(fact) : undefined;
}

export function selectConnectionSummaries(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): ConnectionSummary[] {
  return snapshot.runtimeFacts.map(toSummary);
}

export function selectTransportTargets(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
  query?: ConnectionTargetQuery,
): TransportTargetSnapshot[] {
  return snapshot.runtimeFacts
    .map((fact) => fact.target)
    .filter((target) => matchesTarget(target, query))
    .map(cloneTransportTarget);
}

export function selectLastTransportError(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): TransportErrorSnapshot | undefined {
  return snapshot.lastError ? cloneTransportError(snapshot.lastError) : undefined;
}

export function selectTransportEvents(
  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
): TransportEventSnapshot[] {
  return snapshot.events.map(cloneTransportEvent);
}
```

> AGENT

测试显示：
- Adapter 的 `connect()` 返回 `{ ok: false, error: {...} }` (没有 `events` 字段)
- 测试期望 `outcome.events` 为 `[]`

但 HEAD 的 `applyAdapterOutcome` 会创建合成错误事件，然后收集在它之前和之后的事件。让我来追踪一下：

在 HEAD 中，当 `connect()` 被调用时：
1. `state.upsertConfig(normalized.config)` — 写入配置
2. `state.applyEvent({ kind: 'connect-requested', ... })` — 创建一个事件
3. adapter 返回 `{ ok: false, error: {...} }` (没有 `events`)
4. `applyAdapterOutcome` 运行：
   - `beforeLength = state.getSnapshot().events.length` (= 1，因为 `connect-requested` 事件)
   - `applyEvents(command.events ?? [])` — `command.events` 是 `undefined`，所以 `??` 给出 `[]`，没有应用任何事件
   - `!command.ok && command.events === undefined` → true → `state.applyEvent(toErrorEvent(...))` — 添加错误事件
   - `collectEventsAfter(beforeLength)` — 收集索引 1 及之后的事件 = `[error event]`
   - 返回 `events: [error event]`

但是测试期望 `events` 为 `[]`。这意味着测试已被之前的对话修改过。让我检查一下 HEAD 上的内容：

     - 删除 core/legacy.ts

  **Frame 完成后**: `pnpm -C rewrite vitest run` 确认全绿

  ### Connection 改动清单

  4. **C-S1**: 删除 core/clone.ts
     - ⚠️ 此文件有 16 处 conditional spread（省略 undefined key）
     - structuredClone 会保留 undefined key，语义不同
     - **策略**: 不删文件，改为内部统一用 `{ ...obj }` 浅拷贝 + 对嵌套对象逐层 spread
     - 或者保留文件只删除冗余的 conditional spread（改为简单 spread，因为 TypeScript 类型已保证字段存在性）
     - 逐函数确认：哪些 conditional spread 保护的是真正可能 undefined 的字段 vs 类型已保证必填的字段

  5. **C-S2**: 删除 selectors/ 目录
     - ⚠️ selectors 包含 ~50% 业务逻辑，不是纯透传：
       - selectConnectionSummaries: 有 toSummary 投影（49-63行）
       - selectTransportTargets: 有 matchesTarget 过滤（38-47行）
       - selectReconnectStatus: 有计算逻辑（126-142行）
     - **策略**: 将这三个有逻辑的 selector 内联到 services/connection-service.ts 的 createConnectionReader 中
     - 纯 clone 的 selector（selectConnectionSnapshot, selectTransportConfigs 等）直接删除
     - ConnectionSummary / ConnectionTargetQuery / ReconnectStatus 类型移到 core/types.ts
     - 更新 connection/index.ts 不再 export selector 函数，只 export 类型

  6. **C-R1**: connect 入参收紧
     - connect(config: unknown) → connect(config: TransportConfig)
     - 内部 normalizeTransportConfig 不变

  7. **C-R2**: 原子化 connect
     - 当前流程：先 upsertConfig → 再 adapter.connect → 失败后幽灵 config 留在 state
     - 改为：先 adapter.connect → 成功后 upsertConfig + applyEvent
     - 失败时不写任何 state，直接返回失败结果

  8. **C-R3**: 新增 discoverResources
     - ConnectionTransportAdapter 接口新增可选 discoverResources?() 方法
     - ConnectionService 新增 discoverResources() 方法，委托给 adapter
     - 无 adapter 支持时返回空数组

  9. **C-R4**: BaseTransportConfig 新增 autoConnect?: boolean

  **Connection 完成后**: `pnpm -C rewrite vitest run` 确认全绿

```
1	import { compileExpression } from '@/shared/expression';
2	import {
3	  EXPRESSION_SOURCE_TYPES,
4	  type ConditionalExpressionDefinition,
5	  type ExpressionDefinition,
6	  type ExpressionVariableDefinition,
7	  type ValidationIssue,
8	  type ValidationResult,
9	} from './types';
10	import { createIssue, hasText, isOneOf, toResult } from './validation-utils';
11	
12	const EXPRESSION_KEYWORDS = new Set([
13	  'true',
14	  'false',
15	  'null',
16	  'undefined',
17	  'Math',
18	  'sin',
19	  'cos',
20	  'tan',
21	  'log',
22	  'exp',
23	  'sqrt',
24	  'abs',
25	  'floor',
26	  'ceil',
27	  'round',
28	  'max',
29	  'min',
30	  'pow',
31	]);
32	
33	const DANGEROUS_EXPRESSION_PATTERNS: readonly RegExp[] = [
34	  /\beval\s*\(/,
35	  /\bFunction\s*\(/,
36	  /\bsetTimeout\s*\(/,
37	  /\bsetInterval\s*\(/,
38	  /\bwindow\./,
39	  /\bdocument\./,
40	  /\bprocess\./,
41	  /\brequire\s*\(/,
42	  /\bimport\s+/,
43	  /\bexport\s+/,
44	];
45	
46	function validateExpressionSyntax(value: string, path: string): ValidationIssue[] {
47	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
48	
49	  if (!hasText(value)) {
50	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.empty', path, '表达式不能为空'));
51	    return issues;
52	  }
53	
54	  const compileResult = compileExpression(value);
55	  if (!compileResult.success) {
56	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.compile', path, `表达式语法错误: ${compileResult.error}`));
57	  }
58	
59	  if (DANGEROUS_EXPRESSION_PATTERNS.some((pattern) => pattern.test(value))) {
60	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.unsafe', path, '表达式包含不允许的运行时代码'));
61	  }
62	
63	  return issues;
64	}
65	
66	function extractExpressionIdentifiers(value: string): string[] {
67	  const matches = value.match(/\b[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*\b/g) ?? [];
68	  return Array.from(new Set(matches.filter((match) => !EXPRESSION_KEYWORDS.has(match))));
69	}
70	
71	function validateVariable(
72	  variable: ExpressionVariableDefinition,
73	  path: string,
74	  seenIdentifiers: Set<string>,
75	): ValidationIssue[] {
76	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
77	
78	  if (!/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$/.test(variable.identifier)) {
79	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.variableIdentifier', path, '变量标识符必须是合法标识符'));
80	  }
81	
82	  if (seenIdentifiers.has(variable.identifier)) {
83	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.variableDuplicate', path, '变量标识符不能重复'));
84	  }
85	  seenIdentifiers.add(variable.identifier);
86	
87	  if (!isOneOf(variable.sourceType, EXPRESSION_SOURCE_TYPES)) {
88	    issues.push(
89	      createIssue('expression.variableSourceUnsupported', path, '变量来源类型不在本轮 pilot 范围内'),
90	    );
91	    return issues;
92	  }
93	
94	  if (variable.sourceType === 'frame_field') {
95	    if (!hasText(variable.frameId)) {
96	      issues.push(createIssue('expression.variableFrameMissing', `${path}.frameId`, '帧字段来源必须指定帧 ID'));
97	    }
98	    if (!hasText(variable.fieldId)) {
99	      issues.push(createIssue('expression.variableFieldMissing', `${path}.fieldId`, '帧字段来源必须指定字段 ID'));
100	    }
101	    return issues;
102	  }
103	
104	  if (!hasText(variable.sourceId) && !hasText(variable.fieldId)) {
105	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.variableSourceMissing', path, '变量来源必须指定 sourceId 或 fieldId'));
106	  }
107	
108	  return issues;
109	}
110	
111	function validateConditionalExpression(
112	  expression: ConditionalExpressionDefinition,
113	  path: string,
114	  variableIdentifiers: Set<string>,
115	): ValidationIssue[] {
116	  const issues = [
117	    ...validateExpressionSyntax(expression.condition, `${path}.condition`),
118	    ...validateExpressionSyntax(expression.expression, `${path}.expression`),
119	  ];
120	
121	  for (const expressionKey of ['condition', 'expression'] as const) {
122	    for (const usedIdentifier of extractExpressionIdentifiers(expression[expressionKey])) {
123	      if (!variableIdentifiers.has(usedIdentifier)) {
124	        issues.push(
125	          createIssue(
126	            'expression.variableUndefined',
127	            `${path}.${expressionKey}`,
128	            `表达式引用了未定义变量 ${usedIdentifier}`,
129	          ),
130	        );
131	      }
132	    }
133	  }
134	
135	  return issues;
136	}
137	
138	export function validateExpressionDefinition(
139	  expression: ExpressionDefinition | undefined,
140	  path = 'expressionConfig',
141	): ValidationResult {
142	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
143	
144	  if (!expression) {
145	    return toResult([createIssue('expression.missing', path, '表达式字段必须配置表达式定义')]);
146	  }
147	
148	  if (!Array.isArray(expression.expressions) || expression.expressions.length === 0) {
149	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.expressionsEmpty', `${path}.expressions`, '至少需要一个表达式'));
150	  }
151	
152	  if (!Array.isArray(expression.variables)) {
153	    issues.push(createIssue('expression.variablesInvalid', `${path}.variables`, '变量映射必须是数组'));
154	    return toResult(issues);
155	  }
156	
157	  const seenIdentifiers = new Set<string>();
158	  for (const [index, variable] of expression.variables.entries()) {
159	    issues.push(...validateVariable(variable, `${path}.variables.${index}`, seenIdentifiers));
160	  }
161	
162	  for (const [index, item] of expression.expressions.entries()) {
163	    issues.push(...validateConditionalExpression(item, `${path}.expressions.${index}`, seenIdentifiers));
164	  }
165	
166	  return toResult(issues);
167	}
168
```

```
1	import {
2	  CHECKSUM_METHODS,
3	  DATA_PARTICIPATION_TYPES,
4	  FRAME_DATA_TYPES,
5	  FRAME_DIRECTIONS,
6	  FRAME_INPUT_TYPES,
7	  IDENTIFIER_LOGIC_OPERATORS,
8	  IDENTIFIER_RULE_OPERATORS,
9	  type ChecksumMethod,
10	  type DataParticipationType,
11	  type ExpressionDefinition,
12	  type ExpressionVariableDefinition,
13	  type FrameAsset,
14	  type FrameDataType,
15	  type FrameDirection,
16	  type FrameFieldDefinition,
17	  type FrameInputType,
18	  type FrameOptionDefinition,
19	  type IdentifierRule,
20	  type ValidationIssue,
21	  type LegacyFrameMigrationResult,
22	} from './types';
23	import { validateFrameAssetCollection } from './validation-frame';
24	
25	type UnknownRecord = Record<string, unknown>;
26	
27	function issue(
28	  code: string,
29	  path: string,
30	  message: string,
31	  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',
32	): ValidationIssue {
33	  return { code, path, message, severity };
34	}
35	
36	function isRecord(value: unknown): value is UnknownRecord {
37	  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
38	}
39	
40	function stringValue(value: unknown, fallback = ''): string {
41	  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : fallback;
42	}
43	
44	function booleanValue(value: unknown, fallback: boolean): boolean {
45	  return typeof value === 'boolean' ? value : fallback;
46	}
47	
48	function isStringOption<T extends readonly string[]>(value: unknown, options: T): value is T[number] {
49	  return typeof value === 'string' && (options as readonly string[]).includes(value);
50	}
51	
52	function unknownValueLabel(value: unknown): string {
53	  if (typeof value === 'string' && value.length > 0) {
54	    return value;
55	  }
56	
57	  if (value === undefined) {
58	    return 'undefined';
59	  }
60	
61	  if (value === null) {
62	    return 'null';
63	  }
64	
65	  return String(value);
66	}
67	
68	function numberValue(value: unknown, fallback: number): number {
69	  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : fallback;
70	}
71	
72	function coerceToNumber(value: unknown, fallback: number): number {
73	  if (typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value)) return value;
74	  if (typeof value === 'string') {
75	    const n = Number(value);
76	    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
77	  }
78	  return fallback;
79	}
80	
81	function normalizeDirection(
82	  value: unknown,
83	  path: string,
84	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
85	): FrameDirection {
86	  if (isStringOption(value, FRAME_DIRECTIONS)) {
87	    return value;
88	  }
89	
90	  issues.push(
91	    issue(
92	      'legacy.directionUnsupported',
93	      path,
94	      `旧 frame direction 不受支持: ${unknownValueLabel(value)}`,
95	    ),
96	  );
97	  return stringValue(value) as FrameDirection;
98	}
99	
100	function normalizeDataType(
101	  value: unknown,
102	  path: string,
103	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
104	): FrameDataType {
105	  if (isStringOption(value, FRAME_DATA_TYPES)) {
106	    return value;
107	  }
108	
109	  issues.push(
110	    issue(
111	      'legacy.dataTypeUnsupported',
112	      path,
113	      `旧 field dataType 不受支持: ${unknownValueLabel(value)}`,
114	    ),
115	  );
116	  return stringValue(value) as FrameDataType;
117	}
118	
119	function normalizeInputType(
120	  value: unknown,
121	  path: string,
122	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
123	): FrameInputType {
124	  if (isStringOption(value, FRAME_INPUT_TYPES)) {
125	    return value;
126	  }
127	
128	  issues.push(
129	    issue(
130	      'legacy.inputTypeUnsupported',
131	      path,
132	      `旧 field inputType 不受支持: ${unknownValueLabel(value)}`,
133	    ),
134	  );
135	  return stringValue(value) as FrameInputType;
136	}
137	
138	function normalizeDataParticipationType(
139	  value: unknown,
140	  path: string,
141	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
142	): DataParticipationType {
143	  if (value === undefined) {
144	    issues.push(
145	      issue(
146	        'legacy.dataParticipationDefaulted',
147	        path,
148	        '旧 field 缺少 dataParticipationType，兼容迁移为 direct',
149	        'warning',
150	      ),
151	    );
152	    return 'direct';
153	  }
154	
155	  if (isStringOption(value, DATA_PARTICIPATION_TYPES)) {
156	    return value;
157	  }
158	
159	  issues.push(
160	    issue(
161	      'legacy.dataParticipationUnsupported',
162	      path,
163	      `旧 field dataParticipationType 不受支持: ${unknownValueLabel(value)}`,
164	    ),
165	  );
166	  return stringValue(value) as DataParticipationType;
167	}
168	
169	function normalizeOptions(value: unknown): FrameOptionDefinition[] {
170	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
171	    return [];
172	  }
173	
174	  return value.filter(isRecord).map((option) => ({
175	    value: stringValue(option.value),
176	    label: stringValue(option.label),
177	    ...(typeof option.isDefault === 'boolean' ? { isDefault: option.isDefault } : {}),
178	  }));
179	}
180	
181	function normalizeIdentifierRule(
182	  value: unknown,
183	  path: string,
184	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
185	): IdentifierRule | undefined {
186	  if (!isRecord(value)) {
187	    issues.push(issue('legacy.identifierRuleInvalid', path, '旧标识规则条目必须是对象'));
188	    return undefined;
189	  }
190	
191	  const operatorRaw = stringValue(value.operator);
192	  const operator = isStringOption(operatorRaw, IDENTIFIER_RULE_OPERATORS) ? operatorRaw : undefined;
193	  if (operatorRaw && !operator) {
194	    issues.push(
195	      issue('legacy.identifierRuleOperatorUnsupported', `${path}.operator`, `旧标识规则 operator 不在枚举内: ${operatorRaw}`, 'warning'),
196	    );
197	  }
198	
199	  const logicOperatorRaw = stringValue(value.logicOperator);
200	  const logicOperator = isStringOption(logicOperatorRaw, IDENTIFIER_LOGIC_OPERATORS) ? logicOperatorRaw : undefined;
201	  if (logicOperatorRaw && !logicOperator) {
202	    issues.push(
203	      issue('legacy.identifierRuleLogicOperatorUnsupported', `${path}.logicOperator`, `旧标识规则 logicOperator 不在枚举内: ${logicOperatorRaw}`, 'warning'),
204	    );
205	  }
206	
207	  return {
208	    startIndex: coerceToNumber(value.startIndex, 0),
209	    endIndex: coerceToNumber(value.endIndex, 0),
210	    operator: operator ?? 'eq',
211	    value: stringValue(value.value),
212	    logicOperator: logicOperator ?? 'and',
213	  };
214	}
215	
216	function normalizeExpression(value: unknown): ExpressionDefinition | undefined {
217	  if (!isRecord(value)) {
218	    return undefined;
219	  }
220	
221	  const expressions = Array.isArray(value.expressions)
222	    ? value.expressions.filter(isRecord).map((expression) => ({
223	        condition: stringValue(expression.condition),
224	        expression: stringValue(expression.expression),
225	      }))
226	    : [];
227	
228	  const variables: ExpressionVariableDefinition[] = Array.isArray(value.variables)
229	    ? value.variables.filter(isRecord).map((variable) => ({
230	        identifier: stringValue(variable.identifier),
231	        sourceType: stringValue(variable.sourceType),
232	        sourceId: stringValue(variable.sourceId),
233	        frameId: stringValue(variable.frameId),
234	        fieldId: stringValue(variable.fieldId),
235	      }))
236	    : [];
237	
238	  return { expressions, variables };
239	}
240	
241	function normalizeField(
242	  value: unknown,
243	  frameIndex: number,
244	  index: number,
245	  issues: ValidationIssue[],
246	): FrameFieldDefinition | undefined {
247	  if (!isRecord(value)) {
248	    issues.push(issue('legacy.fieldInvalid', `legacy.${frameIndex}.fields.${index}`, '旧 field 条目必须是对象'));
249	    return undefined;
250	  }
251	
252	  const fieldPath = `legacy.${frameIndex}.fields.${index}`;
253	  const dataType = normalizeDataType(value.dataType, `${fieldPath}.dataType`, issues);
254	  const inputType = normalizeInputType(value.inputType, `${fieldPath}.inputType`, issues);
255	  const dataParticipationType = normalizeDataParticipationType(
256	    value.dataParticipationType,
257	    `${fieldPath}.dataParticipationType`,
258	    issues,
259	  );
260	
261	  const field: FrameFieldDefinition = {
262	    id: stringValue(value.id, `legacy-field-${index + 1}`),
263	    name: stringValue(value.name),
264	    dataType,
265	    length: numberValue(value.length, dataType === 'bytes' ? 1 : 0),
266	    description: stringValue(value.description),
267	    defaultValue: stringValue(value.defaultValue),
268	    inputType,
269	    configurable: booleanValue(value.configurable, false),
270	    options: normalizeOptions(value.options),
271	    dataParticipationType,
272	  };
273	
274	  if (isRecord(value.validOption)) {
275	    const checksumMethodRaw = stringValue(value.validOption.checksumMethod);
276	    const checksumMethod: ChecksumMethod | undefined = isStringOption(checksumMethodRaw, CHECKSUM_METHODS)
277	      ? checksumMethodRaw
278	      : undefined;
279	    if (checksumMethodRaw && !checksumMethod) {
280	      issues.push(
281	        issue(
282	          'legacy.checksumMethodUnsupported',
283	          `${fieldPath}.validOption.checksumMethod`,
284	          `旧 checksumMethod 不在枚举内: ${checksumMethodRaw}`,
285	          'warning',
286	        ),
287	      );
288	    }
289	
290	    field.validOption = {
291	      isChecksum: booleanValue(value.validOption.isChecksum, false),
292	      startFieldIndex: coerceToNumber(value.validOption.startFieldIndex, 0),
293	      endFieldIndex: coerceToNumber(value.validOption.endFieldIndex, 0),
294	      ...(checksumMethod ? { checksumMethod } : {}),
295	    };
296	  }
297	
298	  if (inputType === 'expression') {
299	    const expressionConfig = normalizeExpression(value.expressionConfig);
300	    if (expressionConfig) {
301	      field.expressionConfig = expressionConfig;
302	    }
303	  }
304	
305	  if (typeof value.bigEndian === 'boolean') {
306	    field.bigEndian = value.bigEndian;
307	  }
308	
309	  if (typeof value.isASCII === 'boolean') {
310	    field.isASCII = value.isASCII;
311	  }
312	
313	  if (value.factor !== undefined) {
314	    field.factor = coerceToNumber(value.factor, 1);
315	  }
316	
317	  return field;
318	}
319	
320	export function normalizeFrame(value: unknown, index: number, issues: ValidationIssue[]): FrameAsset | undefined {
321	  if (!isRecord(value)) {
322	    issues.push(issue('legacy.frameInvalid', `legacy.${index}`, '旧 frame 条目必须是对象'));
323	    return undefined;
324	  }
325	
326	  const rawFields = Array.isArray(value.fields) ? value.fields : [];
327	  if (!Array.isArray(value.fields)) {
328	    issues.push(issue('legacy.fieldsInvalid', `legacy.${index}.fields`, '旧 frame 缺少 fields 数组'));
329	  }
330	
331	  const direction = normalizeDirection(value.direction, `legacy.${index}.direction`, issues);
332	
333	  const frame: FrameAsset = {
334	    id: stringValue(value.id, `legacy-frame-${index + 1}`),
335	    name: stringValue(value.name),
336	    direction,
337	    fields: rawFields
338	      .map((field, fieldIndex) => normalizeField(field, index, fieldIndex, issues))
339	      .filter((field): field is FrameFieldDefinition => field !== undefined),
340	    description: stringValue(value.description),
341	    frameType: stringValue(value.frameType),
342	    protocol: stringValue(value.protocol),
343	    isFavorite: booleanValue(value.isFavorite, false),
344	    identifierRules: Array.isArray(value.identifierRules)
345	      ? value.identifierRules
346	          .map((rule, ruleIndex) => normalizeIdentifierRule(rule, `legacy.${index}.identifierRules.${ruleIndex}`, issues))
347	          .filter((rule): rule is IdentifierRule => rule !== undefined)
348	      : [],
349	  };
350	
351	  if (isRecord(value.options)) {
352	    frame.options = {
353	      autoChecksum: booleanValue(value.options.autoChecksum, true),
354	      bigEndian: booleanValue(value.options.bigEndian, false),
355	      includeLengthField: booleanValue(value.options.includeLengthField, false),
356	    };
357	  }
358	
359	  if (typeof value.createdAt === 'string') {
360	    frame.createdAt = value.createdAt;
361	  }
362	
363	  if (typeof value.updatedAt === 'string') {
364	    frame.updatedAt = value.updatedAt;
365	  }
366	
367	  return frame;
368	}
369	
370	export function isRecordCheck(value: unknown): value is UnknownRecord {
371	  return isRecord(value);
372	}
373	
374	export function createIssue(
375	  code: string,
376	  path: string,
377	  message: string,
378	  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',
379	): ValidationIssue {
380	  return issue(code, path, message, severity);
381	}
382	
383	export function isLegacyFrameConfigJson(value: unknown): boolean {
384	  return Array.isArray(value) && value.every((item) => isRecordCheck(item) && Array.isArray(item.fields));
385	}
386	
387	export function migrateLegacyFrameConfig(value: unknown): LegacyFrameMigrationResult {
388	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
389	
390	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
391	    return {
392	      recognized: false,
393	      frames: [],
394	      issues: [createIssue('legacy.rootInvalid', 'legacy', '旧 frame 导入内容必须是数组')],
395	    };
396	  }
397	
398	  const frames = value
399	    .map((item, index) => normalizeFrame(item, index, issues))
400	    .filter((frame): frame is FrameAsset => frame !== undefined);
401	
402	  const validation = validateFrameAssetCollection(frames);
403	
404	  return {
405	    recognized: true,
406	    frames,
407	    issues: [...issues, ...validation.issues],
408	  };
409	}
410
```

```
1	import {
2	  FRAME_DIRECTIONS,
3	  IDENTIFIER_LOGIC_OPERATORS,
4	  IDENTIFIER_RULE_OPERATORS,
5	  type FrameAsset,
6	  type ValidationIssue,
7	  type ValidationResult,
8	} from './types';
9	import { validateFrameField } from './validation-field';
10	import { createIssue, hasText, isOneOf, toResult } from './validation-utils';
11	
12	export function validateFrameAsset(frame: FrameAsset, path = 'frame'): ValidationResult {
13	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
14	
15	  if (!hasText(frame.id)) {
16	    issues.push(createIssue('frame.idMissing', `${path}.id`, '帧 ID 不能为空'));
17	  }
18	
19	  if (!hasText(frame.name)) {
20	    issues.push(createIssue('frame.nameMissing', `${path}.name`, '帧名称不能为空'));
21	  }
22	
23	  if (!isOneOf(frame.direction, FRAME_DIRECTIONS)) {
24	    issues.push(createIssue('frame.directionUnsupported', `${path}.direction`, '帧方向必须是 send 或 receive'));
25	  }
26	
27	  if (!Array.isArray(frame.fields) || frame.fields.length === 0) {
28	    issues.push(createIssue('frame.fieldsMissing', `${path}.fields`, '帧至少需要一个字段'));
29	    return toResult(issues);
30	  }
31	
32	  const names = new Map<string, number>();
33	  for (const [index, field] of frame.fields.entries()) {
34	    issues.push(...validateFrameField(field, `${path}.fields.${index}`).issues);
35	
36	    const fieldName = field.name.trim();
37	    if (!fieldName) {
38	      continue;
39	    }
40	
41	    const count = names.get(fieldName) ?? 0;
42	    names.set(fieldName, count + 1);
43	  }
44	
45	  for (const [name, count] of names.entries()) {
46	    if (count > 1) {
47	      issues.push(createIssue('frame.fieldNameDuplicate', `${path}.fields`, `字段名称必须唯一: ${name}`));
48	    }
49	  }
50	
51	  if (frame.identifierRules) {
52	    for (const [index, rule] of frame.identifierRules.entries()) {
53	      const rulePath = `${path}.identifierRules.${index}`;
54	      if (!Number.isInteger(rule.startIndex) || rule.startIndex < 0) {
55	        issues.push(createIssue('frame.identifierRuleStartIndex', `${rulePath}.startIndex`, '标识规则 startIndex 必须是非负整数'));
56	      }
57	      if (!Number.isInteger(rule.endIndex) || rule.endIndex < 0) {
58	        issues.push(createIssue('frame.identifierRuleEndIndex', `${rulePath}.endIndex`, '标识规则 endIndex 必须是非负整数'));
59	      }
60	      if (!isOneOf(rule.operator, IDENTIFIER_RULE_OPERATORS)) {
61	        issues.push(createIssue('frame.identifierRuleOperator', `${rulePath}.operator`, '标识规则 operator 不在枚举内'));
62	      }
63	      if (!isOneOf(rule.logicOperator, IDENTIFIER_LOGIC_OPERATORS)) {
64	        issues.push(createIssue('frame.identifierRuleLogicOperator', `${rulePath}.logicOperator`, '标识规则 logicOperator 不在枚举内'));
65	      }
66	    }
67	  }
68	
69	  return toResult(issues);
70	}
71	
72	export function validateFrameAssetCollection(frames: readonly FrameAsset[]): ValidationResult {
73	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
74	  const ids = new Map<string, number>();
75	
76	  for (const [index, frame] of frames.entries()) {
77	    issues.push(...validateFrameAsset(frame, `frames.${index}`).issues);
78	
79	    const id = frame.id.trim();
80	    if (!id) {
81	      continue;
82	    }
83	
84	    ids.set(id, (ids.get(id) ?? 0) + 1);
85	  }
86	
87	  for (const [id, count] of ids.entries()) {
88	    if (count > 1) {
89	      issues.push(createIssue('frame.idDuplicate', 'frames', `帧 ID 必须唯一: ${id}`));
90	    }
91	  }
92	
93	  return toResult(issues);
94	}
95	
```

```
1	import {
2	  migrateLegacyFrameConfig,
3	  validateFrameAssetCollection,
4	  type FrameAsset,
5	  type LegacyFrameMigrationResult,
6	  type ReadonlyFrameAsset,
7	  type ValidationIssue,
8	  type ValidationResult,
9	} from '../core';
10	import {
11	  findFrameAssets,
12	  getFrameAsset,
13	  getSelectedFrameAsset,
14	  listFrameAssetSummaries,
15	  selectFieldReferenceOptions,
16	  selectFrameReferenceOptions,
17	  type FrameAssetQuery,
18	  type FrameAssetSnapshot,
19	  type FrameAssetSummary,
20	  type FrameFieldReference,
21	  type FrameFieldReferenceQuery,
22	  type FrameReferenceOption,
23	} from '../selectors';
24	import { createFrameState, type FrameStateContainer, type FrameStateSnapshot } from '../state';
25	
26	export interface FrameAssetReader {
27	  getSnapshot(): FrameAssetSnapshot;
28	  listFrames(query?: FrameAssetQuery): FrameAssetSummary[];
29	  findFrames(query?: FrameAssetQuery): ReadonlyFrameAsset[];
30	  getFrame(frameId: string): ReadonlyFrameAsset | undefined;
31	  getSelectedFrame(): ReadonlyFrameAsset | undefined;
32	  listFrameReferences(query?: FrameAssetQuery): FrameReferenceOption[];
33	  listFieldReferences(query?: FrameFieldReferenceQuery): FrameFieldReference[];
34	}
35	
36	export interface FrameAssetOperationResult {
37	  readonly ok: boolean;
38	  readonly validation: ValidationResult;
39	  readonly snapshot: FrameStateSnapshot;
40	}
41	
42	export interface FrameLegacyLoadResult extends FrameAssetOperationResult {
43	  readonly migration: LegacyFrameMigrationResult;
44	}
45	
46	export interface FrameAssetService extends FrameAssetReader {
47	  replaceFrames(frames: readonly FrameAsset[], selectedFrameId?: string): FrameAssetOperationResult;
48	  loadLegacyFrameConfig(value: unknown, selectedFrameId?: string): FrameLegacyLoadResult;
49	  selectFrame(frameId: string | undefined): FrameAssetOperationResult;
50	  upsertFrame(frame: FrameAsset): FrameAssetOperationResult;
51	  removeFrame(frameId: string): FrameAssetOperationResult;
52	}
53	
54	function hasError(issues: readonly ValidationIssue[]): boolean {
55	  return issues.some((issue) => issue.severity === 'error');
56	}
57	
58	function validResult(): ValidationResult {
59	  return {
60	    valid: true,
61	    issues: [],
62	  };
63	}
64	
65	function issue(
66	  code: string,
67	  path: string,
68	  message: string,
69	  severity: ValidationIssue['severity'] = 'error',
70	): ValidationIssue {
71	  return { code, path, message, severity };
72	}
73	
74	function invalidResult(issues: ValidationIssue[]): ValidationResult {
75	  return {
76	    valid: false,
77	    issues,
78	  };
79	}
80	
81	export function createFrameAssetReader(snapshotProvider: () => FrameAssetSnapshot): FrameAssetReader {
82	  return {
83	    getSnapshot() {
84	      const snapshot = snapshotProvider();
85	      return {
86	        frames: findFrameAssets(snapshot),
87	        ...(snapshot.selectedFrameId ? { selectedFrameId: snapshot.selectedFrameId } : {}),
88	      };
89	    },
90	
91	    listFrames(query) {
92	      return listFrameAssetSummaries(snapshotProvider(), query);
93	    },
94	
95	    findFrames(query) {
96	      return findFrameAssets(snapshotProvider(), query);
97	    },
98	
99	    getFrame(frameId) {
100	      return getFrameAsset(snapshotProvider(), frameId);
101	    },
102	
103	    getSelectedFrame() {
104	      return getSelectedFrameAsset(snapshotProvider());
105	    },
106	
107	    listFrameReferences(query) {
108	      return selectFrameReferenceOptions(snapshotProvider(), query);
109	    },
110	
111	    listFieldReferences(query) {
112	      return selectFieldReferenceOptions(snapshotProvider(), query);
113	    },
114	  };
115	}
116	
117	export function createFrameAssetService(
118	  state: FrameStateContainer = createFrameState(),
119	): FrameAssetService {
120	  const reader = createFrameAssetReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
121	
122	  return {
123	    ...reader,
124	
125	    replaceFrames(frames, selectedFrameId) {
126	      const validation = validateFrameAssetCollection(frames);
127	      if (!validation.valid) {
128	        return {
129	          ok: false,
130	          validation,
131	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
132	        };
133	      }
134	
135	      return {
136	        ok: true,
137	        validation,
138	        snapshot: state.replaceFrames(frames, selectedFrameId),
139	      };
140	    },
141	
142	    loadLegacyFrameConfig(value, selectedFrameId) {
143	      const migration = migrateLegacyFrameConfig(value);
144	      if (!migration.recognized || hasError(migration.issues)) {
145	        return {
146	          ok: false,
147	          validation: {
148	            valid: !hasError(migration.issues),
149	            issues: migration.issues,
150	          },
151	          migration,
152	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
153	        };
154	      }
155	
156	      return {
157	        ok: true,
158	        validation: {
159	          valid: true,
160	          issues: migration.issues,
161	        },
162	        migration,
163	        snapshot: state.replaceFrames(migration.frames, selectedFrameId),
164	      };
165	    },
166	
167	    selectFrame(frameId) {
168	      if (frameId === undefined) {
169	        return {
170	          ok: true,
171	          validation: validResult(),
172	          snapshot: state.selectFrame(undefined),
173	        };
174	      }
175	
176	      if (!getFrameAsset(state.getSnapshot(), frameId)) {
177	        return {
178	          ok: false,
179	          validation: invalidResult([
180	            issue('frame.selectionMissing', 'selectedFrameId', `未找到帧: ${frameId}`),
181	          ]),
182	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
183	        };
184	      }
185	
186	      return {
187	        ok: true,
188	        validation: validResult(),
189	        snapshot: state.selectFrame(frameId),
190	      };
191	    },
192	
193	    upsertFrame(frame) {
194	      const validation = validateFrameAssetCollection([frame]);
195	      if (!validation.valid) {
196	        return {
197	          ok: false,
198	          validation,
199	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
200	        };
201	      }
202	
203	      return {
204	        ok: true,
205	        validation,
206	        snapshot: state.upsertFrame(frame),
207	      };
208	    },
209	
210	    removeFrame(frameId) {
211	      return {
212	        ok: true,
213	        validation: validResult(),
214	        snapshot: state.removeFrame(frameId),
215	      };
216	    },
217	  };
218	}
219	
220	export interface FrameAssetFile {
221	  schemaVersion: 1;
222	  frames: FrameAsset[];
223	}
224	
225	export interface FrameDeserializeResult {
226	  ok: boolean;
227	  frames: FrameAsset[];
228	  issues: ValidationIssue[];
229	}
230	
231	export function serializeFrames(frames: readonly FrameAsset[]): string {
232	  const file: FrameAssetFile = { schemaVersion: 1, frames: [...frames] };
233	  return JSON.stringify(file, null, 2);
234	}
235	
236	export function deserializeFrames(text: string): FrameDeserializeResult {
237	  let parsed: unknown;
238	  try {
239	    parsed = JSON.parse(text);
240	  } catch {
241	    // Try legacy path: the text might be a raw legacy array
242	    return tryLegacyDeserialize();
243	  }
244	
245	  if (isFrameAssetFile(parsed)) {
246	    const validation = validateFrameAssetCollection(parsed.frames);
247	    return {
248	      ok: !hasError(validation.issues),
249	      frames: parsed.frames,
250	      issues: validation.issues,
251	    };
252	  }
253	
254	  // No schemaVersion → legacy array or object
255	  const legacy = migrateLegacyFrameConfig(parsed);
256	  return {
257	    ok: legacy.recognized && !hasError(legacy.issues),
258	    frames: legacy.frames,
259	    issues: legacy.issues,
260	  };
261	}
262	
263	function isFrameAssetFile(value: unknown): value is FrameAssetFile {
264	  return (
265	    typeof value === 'object' &&
266	    value !== null &&
267	    'schemaVersion' in value &&
268	    (value as FrameAssetFile).schemaVersion === 1 &&
269	    'frames' in value &&
270	    Array.isArray((value as FrameAssetFile).frames)
271	  );
272	}
273	
274	function tryLegacyDeserialize(): FrameDeserializeResult {
275	  return {
276	    ok: false,
277	    frames: [],
278	    issues: [issue('deserialize.parseError', 'input', 'JSON 格式错误，无法解析')],
279	  };
280	}
281
```

```
1	import {
2	  DATA_PARTICIPATION_TYPES,
3	  FRAME_DATA_TYPES,
4	  FRAME_INPUT_TYPES,
5	  type FrameDataType,
6	  type FrameFieldDefinition,
7	  type ValidationIssue,
8	  type ValidationResult,
9	} from './types';
10	import { validateExpressionDefinition } from './validation-expression';
11	import { createIssue, hasText, isOneOf, toResult } from './validation-utils';
12	
13	const FIXED_DATA_TYPE_LENGTHS: Partial<Record<FrameDataType, number>> = {
14	  uint8: 1,
15	  int8: 1,
16	  uint16: 2,
17	  int16: 2,
18	  uint32: 4,
19	  int32: 4,
20	  uint64: 8,
21	  int64: 8,
22	  float: 4,
23	  double: 8,
24	};
25	
26	export function validateFrameField(
27	  field: FrameFieldDefinition,
28	  path = 'field',
29	): ValidationResult {
30	  const issues: ValidationIssue[] = [];
31	
32	  if (!hasText(field.id)) {
33	    issues.push(createIssue('field.idMissing', `${path}.id`, '字段 ID 不能为空'));
34	  }
35	
36	  if (!hasText(field.name)) {
37	    issues.push(createIssue('field.nameMissing', `${path}.name`, '字段名称不能为空'));
38	  }
39	
40	  if (!isOneOf(field.dataType, FRAME_DATA_TYPES)) {
41	    issues.push(createIssue('field.dataTypeUnsupported', `${path}.dataType`, '字段类型不在本轮 pilot 范围内'));
42	  }
43	
44	  if (!Number.isInteger(field.length) || field.length <= 0) {
45	    issues.push(createIssue('field.lengthInvalid', `${path}.length`, '字段长度必须是正整数'));
46	  }
47	
48	  const fixedLength = FIXED_DATA_TYPE_LENGTHS[field.dataType];
49	  if (fixedLength !== undefined && field.length !== fixedLength) {
50	    issues.push(
51	      createIssue(
52	        'field.lengthMismatch',
53	        `${path}.length`,
54	        `字段类型 ${field.dataType} 的长度应为 ${fixedLength}`,
55	      ),
56	    );
57	  }
58	
59	  if (!isOneOf(field.inputType, FRAME_INPUT_TYPES)) {
60	    issues.push(createIssue('field.inputTypeUnsupported', `${path}.inputType`, '字段输入类型不受支持'));
61	  }
62	
63	  if (!isOneOf(field.dataParticipationType, DATA_PARTICIPATION_TYPES)) {
64	    issues.push(
65	      createIssue(
66	        'field.dataParticipationUnsupported',
67	        `${path}.dataParticipationType`,
68	        '数据参与类型不受支持',
69	      ),
70	    );
71	  }
72	
73	  if ((field.inputType === 'select' || field.inputType === 'radio') && field.options.length === 0) {
74	    issues.push(createIssue('field.optionsMissing', `${path}.options`, '选择类字段必须提供选项'));
75	  }
76	
77	  if (field.inputType === 'expression') {
78	    issues.push(...validateExpressionDefinition(field.expressionConfig, `${path}.expressionConfig`).issues);
79	  }
80	
81	  return toResult(issues);
82	}
83
```
