```
1	import { defaultNow } from '@/shared';
2	import {
3	  cloneConnectionRuntimeFact,
4	  cloneConnectionStateSnapshot,
5	  cloneTransportConfig,
6	  cloneTransportError,
7	  cloneTransportEvent,
8	  cloneTransportTarget,
9	  createConnectionIssue,
10	  createConnectionValidationOutcome,
11	  normalizeTransportConfig,
12	  type ConnectionRuntimeFact,
13	  type ConnectionStateSnapshot,
14	  type ConnectionSummary,
15	  type ConnectionTargetQuery,
16	  type ConnectionValidationOutcome,
17	  type NormalizedTransportEventInput,
18	  type ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
19	  type TransportConfig,
20	  type TransportErrorSnapshot,
21	  type TransportEventSnapshot,
22	  type TransportTargetSnapshot,
23	} from '../core';
24	import type {
25	  ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome,
26	  ConnectionAdapterErrorInput,
27	  ConnectionAdapterEvent,
28	  ConnectionResourceCandidate,
29	  ConnectionTransportAdapter,
30	  TransportWriteRequest,
31	} from '../adapters';
32	import { createConnectionState, type ConnectionStateContainer } from '../state/connection-state';
33	
34	export interface ReconnectStatus {
35	  readonly connectionId: string;
36	  readonly phase: 'idle' | 'scheduled' | 'connecting' | 'aborting';
37	  readonly attempt: number;
38	  readonly nextAttemptAt?: string;
39	}
40	
41	export interface ConnectionReader {
42	  getSnapshot(): ConnectionStateSnapshot;
43	  listTransportConfigs(): TransportConfig[];
44	  listConnectionFacts(): ConnectionRuntimeFact[];
45	  getConnectionFact(connectionId: string): ConnectionRuntimeFact | undefined;
46	  listConnectionSummaries(): ConnectionSummary[];
47	  listTransportTargets(query?: ConnectionTargetQuery): TransportTargetSnapshot[];
48	  getLastTransportError(): TransportErrorSnapshot | undefined;
49	  listTransportEvents(): TransportEventSnapshot[];
50	  getReconnectStatus(connectionId: string): ReconnectStatus | undefined;
51	}
52	
53	export interface ConnectionOperationOutcome {
54	  readonly ok: boolean;
55	  readonly validation: ConnectionValidationOutcome;
56	  readonly snapshot: ConnectionStateSnapshot;
57	  readonly events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[];
58	  readonly error?: TransportErrorSnapshot;
59	}
60	
61	export interface ConnectionService extends ConnectionReader {
62	  connect(config: TransportConfig): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
63	  disconnect(connectionId: string): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
64	  write(request: TransportWriteRequest): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
65	  drainAdapterEvents(): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
66	  cleanup(): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;
67	  discoverResources(): Promise<readonly ConnectionResourceCandidate[]>;
68	}
69	
70	export interface CreateConnectionServiceOptions {
71	  readonly adapter: ConnectionTransportAdapter;
72	  readonly state?: ConnectionStateContainer;
73	  readonly now?: () => string;
74	}
75	
76	// --- Inline selector helpers (replaces deleted selectors/) ---
77	
78	function matchesTarget(
79	  target: TransportTargetSnapshot,
80	  query: ConnectionTargetQuery | undefined,
81	): boolean {
82	  if (query?.kind && target.kind !== query.kind) {
83	    return false;
84	  }
85	
86	  return query?.availableOnly ? target.available : true;
87	}
88	
89	function toSummary(fact: ConnectionRuntimeFact): ConnectionSummary {
90	  return {
91	    connectionId: fact.connectionId,
92	    kind: fact.kind,
93	    lifecycle: fact.lifecycle,
94	    label: fact.target.label,
95	    routeLabel: fact.target.routeLabel,
96	    available: fact.target.available,
97	    rxBytes: fact.counters.rxBytes,
98	    txBytes: fact.counters.txBytes,
99	    errorCount: fact.counters.errorCount,
100	    ...(fact.lastActivityAt ? { lastActivityAt: fact.lastActivityAt } : {}),
101	    ...(fact.lastError ? { lastError: cloneTransportError(fact.lastError) } : {}),
102	  };
103	}
104	
105	// --- Private helpers ---
106	
107	function validValidation(): ConnectionValidationOutcome {
108	  return createConnectionValidationOutcome([]);
109	}
110	
111	function invalidConfigValidation(): ConnectionValidationOutcome {
112	  return createConnectionValidationOutcome([
113	    createConnectionIssue(
114	      'connection.config.missing',
115	      'config',
116	      'Connection config could not be normalized.',
117	      'error',
118	    ),
119	  ]);
120	}
121	
122	function adapterErrorToSnapshot(
123	  error: ConnectionAdapterErrorInput,
124	  connectionId: string,
125	  occurredAt: string,
126	): TransportErrorSnapshot {
127	  return {
128	    kind: error.kind,
129	    message: error.message,
130	    occurredAt,
131	    connectionId,
132	    ...(error.recoverable !== undefined ? { recoverable: error.recoverable } : {}),
133	  };
134	}
135	
136	function adapterEventToNormalized(
137	  event: ConnectionAdapterEvent,
138	  now: () => string,
139	): NormalizedTransportEventInput {
140	  const occurredAt = event.occurredAt ?? now();
141	  return {
142	    kind: event.kind,
143	    connectionId: event.connectionId,
144	    occurredAt,
145	    ...(event.target ? { target: event.target } : {}),
146	    ...(event.byteLength !== undefined
147	      ? { byteLength: event.byteLength }
148	      : event.bytes
149	        ? { byteLength: event.bytes.length }
150	        : {}),
151	    ...(event.bytes ? { bytes: event.bytes } : {}),
152	    ...(event.error
153	      ? {
154	          error: {
155	            kind: event.error.kind,
156	            message: event.error.message,
157	            ...(event.error.recoverable !== undefined
158	              ? { recoverable: event.error.recoverable }
159	              : {}),
160	          },
161	        }
162	      : {}),
163	  };
164	}
165	
166	function toErrorEvent(
167	  connectionId: string,
168	  error: ConnectionAdapterErrorInput,
169	  occurredAt: string,
170	): NormalizedTransportEventInput {
171	  return {
172	    kind: 'error',
173	    connectionId,
174	    occurredAt,
175	    error,
176	  };
177	}
178	
179	function collectEventsAfter(
180	  beforeLength: number,
181	  snapshot: ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
182	): TransportEventSnapshot[] {
183	  return snapshot.events.slice(beforeLength).map(cloneTransportEvent);
184	}
185	
186	// --- Public factories ---
187	
188	export function createConnectionReader(
189	  snapshotProvider: () => ReadonlyConnectionStateSnapshot,
190	): ConnectionReader {
191	  return {
192	    getSnapshot() {
193	      return cloneConnectionStateSnapshot(snapshotProvider());
194	    },
195	
196	    listTransportConfigs() {
197	      return snapshotProvider().configs.map(cloneTransportConfig);
198	    },
199	
200	    listConnectionFacts() {
201	      return snapshotProvider().runtimeFacts.map(cloneConnectionRuntimeFact);
202	    },
203	
204	    getConnectionFact(connectionId) {
205	      const fact = snapshotProvider().runtimeFacts.find(
206	        (item) => item.connectionId === connectionId,
207	      );
208	      return fact ? cloneConnectionRuntimeFact(fact) : undefined;
209	    },
210	
211	    listConnectionSummaries() {
212	      return snapshotProvider().runtimeFacts.map(toSummary);
213	    },
214	
215	    listTransportTargets(query) {
216	      return snapshotProvider().runtimeFacts
217	        .map((fact) => fact.target)
218	        .filter((target) => matchesTarget(target, query))
219	        .map(cloneTransportTarget);
220	    },
221	
222	    getLastTransportError() {
223	      const snapshot = snapshotProvider();
224	      return snapshot.lastError ? cloneTransportError(snapshot.lastError) : undefined;
225	    },
226	
227	    listTransportEvents() {
228	      return snapshotProvider().events.map(cloneTransportEvent);
229	    },
230	
231	    getReconnectStatus() {
232	      return undefined;
233	    },
234	  };
235	}
236	
237	export function createConnectionService(
238	  options: CreateConnectionServiceOptions,
239	): ConnectionService {
240	  const state = options.state ?? createConnectionState();
241	  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;
242	  const reader = createConnectionReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
243	
244	  function applyEvents(events: readonly ConnectionAdapterEvent[]): void {
245	    for (const event of events) {
246	      state.applyEvent(adapterEventToNormalized(event, now));
247	    }
248	  }
249	
250	  function applyAdapterOutcome(
251	    connectionId: string,
252	    command: ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome,
253	  ): ConnectionOperationOutcome {
254	    const beforeLength = state.getSnapshot().events.length;
255	
256	    applyEvents(command.events ?? []);
257	    if (!command.ok && command.events === undefined) {
258	      state.applyEvent(toErrorEvent(connectionId, command.error, now()));
259	    }
260	
261	    const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
262	    const events = collectEventsAfter(beforeLength, snapshot);
263	    const lastEvent = events[events.length - 1];
264	    const error = !command.ok
265	      ? adapterErrorToSnapshot(command.error, connectionId, lastEvent?.occurredAt ?? now())
266	      : undefined;
267	
268	    return {
269	      ok: command.ok,
270	      validation: command.ok
271	        ? validValidation()
272	        : createConnectionValidationOutcome([
273	            createConnectionIssue(
274	              `connection.adapter.${command.error.kind}`,
275	              'adapter',
276	              command.error.message,
277	              'error',
278	            ),
279	          ]),
280	      snapshot,
281	      events,
282	      ...(error ? { error } : {}),
283	    };
284	  }
285	
286	  return {
287	    ...reader,
288	
289	    async connect(input) {
290	      const normalized = normalizeTransportConfig(input);
291	      if (!normalized.valid || !normalized.config) {
292	        return {
293	          ok: false,
294	          validation: normalized.issues.length > 0 ? normalized : invalidConfigValidation(),
295	          snapshot: state.getSnapshot(),
296	          events: [],
297	        };
298	      }
299	
300	      state.upsertConfig(normalized.config);
301	      state.applyEvent({
302	        kind: 'connect-requested',
303	        connectionId: normalized.config.id,
304	        occurredAt: now(),
305	      });
306	
307	      const command = await options.adapter.connect(normalized.config);
308	      return applyAdapterOutcome(normalized.config.id, command);
309	    },
310	
311	    async disconnect(connectionId) {
312	      state.applyEvent({
313	        kind: 'disconnect-requested',
314	        connectionId,
315	        occurredAt: now(),
316	      });
317	
318	      const command = await options.adapter.disconnect(connectionId);
319	      return applyAdapterOutcome(connectionId, command);
320	    },
321	
322	    async write(request) {
323	      state.applyEvent({
324	        kind: 'write-requested',
325	        connectionId: request.connectionId,
326	        occurredAt: now(),
327	        byteLength: request.bytes.length,
328	      });
329	
330	      const command = await options.adapter.write(request);
331	      return applyAdapterOutcome(request.connectionId, command);
332	    },
333	
334	    async drainAdapterEvents() {
335	      const beforeLength = state.getSnapshot().events.length;
336	      const events = await options.adapter.drainEvents();
337	      applyEvents(events);
338	      const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
339	
340	      const lastError = snapshot.lastError
341	        ? cloneTransportError(snapshot.lastError)
342	        : undefined;
343	
344	      return {
345	        ok: true,
346	        validation: validValidation(),
347	        snapshot,
348	        events: collectEventsAfter(beforeLength, snapshot),
349	        ...(lastError ? { error: lastError } : {}),
350	      };
351	    },
352	
353	    async cleanup() {
354	      for (const fact of state.getSnapshot().runtimeFacts) {
355	        state.applyEvent({
356	          kind: 'cleanup',
357	          connectionId: fact.connectionId,
358	          occurredAt: now(),
359	        });
360	      }
361	
362	      const command = await options.adapter.cleanup();
363	      return applyAdapterOutcome('cleanup', command);
364	    },
365	
366	    async discoverResources() {
367	      if (options.adapter.discoverResources) {
368	        return options.adapter.discoverResources();
369	      }
370	      return [];
371	    },
372	  };
373	}
374	
```

测试期望（另一个对话写的）：
1. `outcome.events` 为 `[]`（原子化：不写 state → 没有事件）
2. `getConnectionFact` 为 `undefined`（原子化：失败不写 state）

但我们的 service 在 `connect` 时先 `upsertConfig` 和 `applyEvent('connect-requested')` 再调 adapter，失败后 `applyAdapterOutcome` 会产生 error event。这是 HEAD 原始行为。

```
53	type FrameOverviewPort = Pick<FrameAssetReader, 'getSelectedFrame' | 'listFrames'>;

64	export const ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS = 100;

67	  readonly connectionAdapter?: ConnectionTransportAdapter;

73	export interface RewriteRuntime {
74	  getOverviewSnapshot(): RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot;
75	  resetSettings(scope?: SettingsResetScope): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult;
76	  readonly features: RewriteWiredFeatures;
77	  routingTick(): Promise<RoutingTickResult>;

83	
84	function createNoOpConnectionAdapter(): ConnectionTransportAdapter {
85	  const accepted = { ok: true as const, events: [] as const };
86	  return {
87	    connect: async () => accepted,
88	    disconnect: async () => accepted,
89	    write: async () => accepted,
90	    cleanup: async () => accepted,
91	    drainEvents: async () => [] as const,
92	  };
93	}
94	
95	function toRuntimeCommandResult(result: SettingsOperationResult): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
96	  return {
97	    ok: result.ok,
98	    issues: result.validation.issues.map((issue) => ({
99	      severity: issue.severity,
100	      code: issue.code,
101	      message: issue.message,
102	    })),
103	  };
104	}
105	
106	export function createRewriteRuntime(
107	  dependencies: RewriteRuntimeDependencies = {},
108	): RewriteRuntime {
109	  const adapter = dependencies.connectionAdapter ?? createNoOpConnectionAdapter();
110	  const wiredFeatures = wireFeatures({ connectionAdapter: adapter });
111	
112	  const frameReader = dependencies.frameReader ?? wiredFeatures.frameReader;
113	  const settingsService = dependencies.settingsService ?? wiredFeatures.settingsService;
114	  const storageReader = dependencies.storageReader ?? wiredFeatures.storageReader;
115	
116	  let destroyed = false;
117	  let tickIntervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
118	
119	  function stopTick(): void {
120	    if (tickIntervalId !== null) {
121	      clearInterval(tickIntervalId);
122	      tickIntervalId = null;
123	    }
124	  }
125	
126	  return {
127	    features: wiredFeatures,
128	
129	    getOverviewSnapshot() {
130	      const frameSummaries = frameReader.listFrames();
131	      const selectedFrame = frameReader.getSelectedFrame();
132	      const recordingSettings = settingsService.getRecordingSettings();
133	      const storageIssue = storageReader.getLastIssue();
134	
135	      return {
136	        frame: {
137	          totalFrames: frameSummaries.length,
138	          totalFields: frameSummaries.reduce((sum, frame) => sum + frame.fieldCount, 0),
139	          selectedFrameName: selectedFrame?.name ?? null,
140	        },
141	        settings: {
142	          autoStartRecording: recordingSettings.autoStartRecording,
143	          csvDefaultOutputPath: recordingSettings.csvDefaultOutputPath,
144	          csvSaveIntervalMinutes: recordingSettings.csvSaveIntervalMinutes,
145	        },
146	        storage: {
147	          localRecordCount: storageReader.listLocalRecords().length,
148	          historyHourCount: storageReader.listHistoryHours().length,
149	          csvMaterialCount: storageReader.listCsvMaterials().length,
150	          legacyMaterialCount: storageReader.listLegacyMaterials().length,
151	          lastIssue: storageIssue
152	            ? {
153	                code: storageIssue.code,
154	                message: storageIssue.message,
155	              }
156	            : null,
157	        },
158	      };
159	    },
160	
161	    resetSettings(scope = 'all') {
162	      return toRuntimeCommandResult(settingsService.reset(scope));
163	    },
164	
165	    routingTick() {
166	      return routingTick(wiredFeatures);
167	    },
168	
169	    startTickDriver(intervalMs = ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS) {
170	      if (tickIntervalId !== null || destroyed) return;
171	      tickIntervalId = setInterval(() => {
172	        routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch(() => {});
173	      }, intervalMs);
174	    },
175	
176	    stopTickDriver() {
177	      stopTick();
178	    },
179	
180	    get isTickDriverRunning() {
181	      return tickIntervalId !== null;
182	    },
183	
184	    destroy() {
185	      if (destroyed) return;
186	      destroyed = true;
187	      stopTick();
188	      wiredFeatures.connectionService.cleanup();
189	      wiredFeatures.receiveEventSourceBridge.clear();
190	    },
191	  };
192	}
193
```

## 剩余问题清单

### 1. bootstrap-integration 测试失败（HEAD 已有）

**文件:** `rewrite/src/runtime/__tests__/bootstrap-integration.spec.ts` 第 27 行
**现象:** `routingTick()` 返回 `ok: false`，因为 receive 没有帧定义
**根因:** `receive/core/frame-matcher.ts` 第 133-143 行：`reference.frames` 为空时返回 `receive.frame.none` issue → 被 `matchIssuesAreConfigErrors` 判为 `config-error` → `outcomeIsOk` 返回 false → `routingTick` 返回失败
**修复方向二选一:**
- A: bootstrap 测试里先给 receive 注入帧定义再推数据
- B: routingTick 对 receive config-error 做容错（空帧定义不应阻断 tick）

### 2. 8 个 reconnect 测试失败（新增功能未完成）

**文件:** `connection/__tests__/connection-reconnect-service.spec.ts`（5个）+ `connection/__tests__/connection-reconnect.spec.ts`（3个）
**现象:** `getReconnectStatus` 返回 undefined、reconnect scheduling 不触发、backoff 计数不对
**根因:** `connection/core/reconnect.ts` 和 service 层 reconnect 逻辑未实现完整，只有接口壳（`getReconnectStatus` 硬返回 undefined）

### 3. C-R2 原子化 connect 待实现

**文件:** `connection/services/connection-service.ts` 的 `connect` 方法
**当前:** 先 `upsertConfig` + `applyEvent('connect-requested')` 再调 adapter，失败后幽灵 config 和 error event 留在 state
**目标:** 先 adapter.connect → 成功后才写 state，失败时返回空结果不污染 state
**前置:** 需先解决问题 1（bootstrap 测试），否则原子化后该测试会更难修
**注意:** `connection-state-service-selector.spec.ts` 第 170-182 行的测试已恢复为匹配当前非原子化行为，实现 C-R2 时需同步更新

### 4. lint 7 errors（非 connection/frame）

全是 `no-unused-vars` 或 `no-explicit-any`，分布在 command-ingress、task、feature-wiring，不是本任务范围。
